### INV-000004 | sec 1 | flujos/flujo-anclas.html | ENTRA
Un ancla es la imagen fija que el modelo de video va a heredar. Todo lo crítico se fija en el still antes de animar: identidad, composición, aspect ratio, texto visible y estado del producto. El video amplifica cualquier ambigüedad del still. Este flujo clasifica cada ancla en nueve dimensiones, elige la técnica de producción según el tipo de acción y el número de personas, y la lleva por lints, generación, crítica y canonización hasta el motion brief.

### INV-000012 | sec 1 | flujos/flujo-anclas.html | ENTRA
Character ref: maestro de identidad. Front, three-quarter, profile, back para Kling Elements; luz pareja, sin sombras duras que el modelo confunda con rasgos, fondo limpio, 1080p o más, sin texto.

### INV-000014 | sec 1 | flujos/flujo-anclas.html | ENTRA
Keyframe FF: primer frame del clip. Fija identidad, layout y texto en imagen; el prompt de video describe solo cómo evoluciona.

### INV-000015 | sec 1 | flujos/flujo-anclas.html | ENTRA
Keyframe LF: último frame. Mismo aspect ratio que el FF o el modelo lo estira; se declara por separado del FF en Seedance.

### INV-000017 | sec 1 | flujos/flujo-anclas.html | SALE | solo fuentes de keyframe para Nano Banana y modelos de video (Kling, Veo, Seedance)

Ensamble y multitud son dos técnicas distintas, no dos tamaños de la misma | SW30 R1 R4 R7 R9 · fixes twins · kling §7 Elements · nano-banana multi-reference · U13

### INV-000043 | sec 2 | flujos/flujo-anclas.html | ENTRA
| D9 | Ángulo y altura de cámara | eye-level · high · low · overhead o top-down · dutch · worm's eye · POV · altura física: a ras de suelo, parachoques, ojos, grúa o aérea, bajo el agua mirando a la superficie | El ángulo es la emoción cuando no hay rostro y la relación de poder cuando lo hay: low es dominio, amenaza o fuerza de lanzamiento; eye-level es paridad; high y top-down es juicio, pequeñez o aftermath; dutch se reserva para un solo beat de inestabilidad. Se elige por la dinámica de poder del beat, nunca por variedad. Un ángulo distinto al spec es hard fail en la crítica | shot-grammar Angle · dramaturgy §6 · animatic §6 · race §10 anti god-angle · kling §3 angle carries emotion · critique-rubric Layer 3

### INV-000046 | sec 2 | flujos/flujo-anclas.html | ENTRA
| A pose sostenida | Eye-level para maestros de identidad; low o high solo si el beat lo pide | Un maestro debe leerse neutro para servir a todos los shots; el ángulo dramático se decide en el cuadro, no en la génesis | Retrato documental con poder declarado en el script | kling §7 source image rules · shot-grammar

### INV-000125 | sec 4 | flujos/flujo-anclas.html | ENTRA
| A pose sostenida | Retrato documental, persona esperando, objeto ancla en reposo cargado, maestro de identidad | Quietud deliberada: cero blur, bordes duros, reloj parado. O vector de postura que solo puede resolverse hacia adelante | Anatomía solo en decisión: nombrar el verbo de la mano. Micro-acción en el cuerpo, una presión ambiental, un motivo. Para maestros: luz pareja, fondo limpio, sin sombras duras | Anatomía en reposo sin verbo, objeto estático sin cambio de estado, "beautiful establishing shot" | Tres detalles presentes; la pose tiene vector o es un maestro de identidad declarado | animatic §4 §5 · dramaturgy §2 §3 · kling §7

### INV-000130 | sec 4 | flujos/flujo-anclas.html | ENTRA
| F multitud anónima | Estadio, manifestación, gente corriendo por la calle, público de concierto. Un grupo con identidades no es tipo F: ver ensamble en D3 | Profundidad en tres planos: héroes nítidos en MG, multitud como textura en FG y BG, rostros fragmentados, siluetas, espaldas, contraluz | La multitud es entorno, no sujetos: se produce como placa con figuras anónimas y sin rostros legibles, después se insertan los héroes canonizados uno por uno. Máximo 3 héroes con identidad; cada uno con su propia referencia y rol. Forzar diferenciación: ropa, pelo y rasgos distintos, movimientos no sincronizados, "no identical clones in the background". Multitud en movimiento, manifestación o carrera callejera: la multitud se trata como textura barrida con las cues de velocidad del tipo C | Pedir N rostros distintos desde texto, clones del héroe entre los extras, caras que se funden, más de 3 identidades sin referencia individual | Contar rostros legibles: todos los legibles son héroes canonizados; ningún extra clona al héroe; los planos FG/MG/BG tienen trabajos distintos | fixes twins/face-blending · kling §7 3+ characters only with own element · U13 · race §3 crowd at periphery · animatic §6 §7 · nano-banana multi-reference · SW30 R1 R7

### INV-000131 | sec 4 | flujos/flujo-anclas.html | ENTRA
Cuatro huecos en las skills instaladas. Los tipos B, D y F, y el grupo ensamble de D3, no tienen sección propia en ninguna fuente. El tipo B se deriva del método de motion-as-still y del consequence prompting; el D de las reglas de atmósfera a contraluz y de fuente motivada; el F de la corrección de gemelos y del límite de personajes de Kling; el ensamble de los límites de referencias por modelo y de la disciplina de roles U13. Las reglas marcadas como inferencia deben canonizarse después de probarse con un caso real, igual que se hizo con el template de rostro.

### INV-000269 | sec 9 | flujos/flujo-anclas.html | ENTRA
Consecuencia para la base de reglas: las ocho dimensiones de la tarjeta de ancla son facetas de consulta. Un brief de "clavadista olímpica entrando al agua, documental, un sujeto, keyframe FF para Kling" filtra por D2 = B, D3 = 1, D4 = documental, D1 = FF, D9 = low a ras de agua, D8 según rostro visible, y trae exactamente las reglas de física por consecuencia, blur parcial, luz dura, template de piel si hay rostro, y las de imagen a video de Kling. Sin la tarjeta, la consulta devuelve el catálogo entero.

### INV-000419 | sec 14 | flujos/flujo-spot.html | ENTRA
Un activo génesis es cualquier sujeto que el pipeline necesita fijar en imagen antes de que exista una referencia de él. Las fuentes distinguen seis clases, y la clase decide modelo, archivo de patrón, bloques del prompt y qué puede heredar de él lo que viene después. Fuentes: image/references/models.md, nano-banana.md sección Image Grounding, patterns/*.md, produccion-visual-sw30 reglas 7 y 10, visual-prompt-forge regla 6, aurora-prompt-linter categorías P / O / L / PR / S.

### INV-000479 | sec 15 | flujos/flujo-spot.html | ENTRA | «Cualquiera; NBP si…»: aplica a cualquier modelo
| Objeto / prop | El objeto ancla, herramienta, vehículo, mobiliario | PR | PROD | Cualquiera; NBP si el objeto tiene física o transparencia | Sí para especies vegetales; no para objetos genéricos | Reaparece en varios shots como motivo (dramaturgy §13), así que se canoniza como una persona. Legible en macro y en plano general | Detalle, macro insert, continuidad del motivo entre clips

### INV-000480 | sec 15 | flujos/flujo-spot.html | ENTRA
Principio de dos pasos. La génesis fija solo la parte del sujeto que lleva la identidad; todo lo que la extiende es un paso posterior con esa génesis adjunta como referencia. En una persona la identidad vive en el rostro, así que el orden es retrato de rostro T1 → cuerpo entero T2 con el rostro como Image 1 → cuadros T3 y T4. En un animal, la cabeza y el pelaje primero, las poses después. En un producto, el hero shot frontal primero, el producto en escena después. Fuentes: SW30 reglas 1, 5 y 11; image/characters.md "Identity Locking"; nano-banana.md, fallos conocidos: los rostros y las manos derivan, y NBP a veces sostiene peor los rostros que NB2.

description: >
  Image prompting skill for Nano Banana (NBP/NB2) and GPT Image 2. Writes ready-to-use
  prompts with model/quality/size recommendations. Use when: "нарисуй", "сгенерируй
  картинку", "image prompt", "промпт для картинки", blog covers, slides, posters,
  product shots, UI mockups, storyboards, character sheets, edit/colorize, style transfer,
  vision analysis, image-to-prompt, nb, NBP, NB2, gpt-image-2, multi-panel grids,
  ecommerce product photography, fashion editorial, food/beverage ads, cinematic portraits.
  Do NOT use for: video (use video skill), 3D models, audio, non-image tasks.

### INV-001999 | sec 196 | skills/image/SKILL.md | ENTRA
# Image Prompting — Nano Banana & GPT Image 2

### INV-002003 | sec 197 | skills/image/SKILL.md | SALE | enruta movimiento y clips (Seedance, Kling, Veo) al skill de video
- **Motion, clips, montage** (Seedance, Kling, Veo, any image-to-video): use the sibling `video` skill. This skill's storyboard and keyframe outputs feed it.

### INV-002010 | sec 199 | skills/image/SKILL.md | ENTRA
Decide: Nano Banana (NB2 or NBP) or GPT Image 2. The choice changes the prompt syntax fundamentally — natural-language paragraphs vs. labeled 5-slot template, quality settings, which features exist (image grounding only on NB, EXACT TEXT discipline only on GPT Image, etc.).

### INV-002013 | sec 200 | skills/image/SKILL.md | SALE | enlace y capacidades de nano-banana.md
- **Nano Banana** → [nano-banana.md](references/nano-banana.md)
  Image grounding for real locations. Extreme aspect ratios (1:8, 8:1, 4:1). Thinking mode. JSON for 5+ elements. Up to 14 reference images. Why you must NOT write `50mm / f-stop / ISO` numbers.

### INV-002014 | sec 200 | skills/image/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- **GPT Image 2** → [gpt-image.md](references/gpt-image.md)
  5-slot template (Scene / Subject / Important Details / Use Case / Constraints). Anti-slop banned-words list. `quality: low / medium / high` as a deliberate fidelity lever. Size constraints (multiples of 16, max 3:1, up to 2560×1440). Two-column edit logic (Change / Preserve / Constraints). Up to 16 reference images with explicit roles.

### INV-002044 | sec 205 | skills/image/SKILL.md | ENTRA
```
Model: <nano-banana-2 | nano-banana-pro | gpt-image-2>
Quality: <low | medium | high>          (only for gpt-image-2)
Size / Ratio: <e.g. 1536×1024 or 16:9>

Prompt:
<the prompt text, ready to copy>

Notes:
- <anything you inferred or assumed because the user did not specify>
```

### INV-002045 | sec 205 | skills/image/SKILL.md | ENTRA
For edits, also include an explicit preserve-list (mandatory for gpt-image-2, recommended for nano-banana):

### INV-002049 | sec 206 | skills/image/SKILL.md | ENTRA
Prefer: ready-to-copy prompts, hex colors, concrete materials, named compositions, model-specific syntax (5-slot for GPT Image, natural prose for Nano Banana).

### INV-002050 | sec 206 | skills/image/SKILL.md | ENTRA
Avoid: tag soup ("cool, modern, 4k"), vague praise ("stunning, epic, masterpiece" — actively hurts GPT Image 2), negative framing ("no people, no cars" — invert to positive), external comparisons ("like Apple ad" — describe the visual properties instead), numerical lens parameters in Nano Banana prompts (it ignores them).

### INV-002053 | sec 207 | skills/image/references/characters.md | ENTRA
# Character Consistency

### INV-002054 | sec 207 | skills/image/references/characters.md | ENTRA | el documento no dice a qué modelo se refiere y image/SKILL.md lo carga en el Paso 4 (L58) sin restringir modelo. «14 (6 high-fidelity)» es exactamente el desglose de NBP: hasta 14 refs (models.md:20, :62) = 6 objetos de alta fidelidad + 5 character + 3 style (nano-banana.md:64-66, :144). Para GPT Image 2 el corpus da hasta 16 con rol (SKILL.md:43, golden-rules.md:93, gpt-image.md:102, models.md:20, :54) y alta fidelidad por defecto (gpt-image.md:96)
Up to 14 reference images (6 high-fidelity). Identity locking across scenes.

### INV-002055 | sec 208 | skills/image/references/characters.md | ENTRA
## Identity Locking

### INV-002056 | sec 208 | skills/image/references/characters.md | ENTRA
```
Using person from [IMAGE REFERENCE]:
Keep facial features exactly the same.
Change: [EXPRESSION / POSE / CLOTHING / SETTING]
Maintain: identity, recognizable features
```

### INV-002057 | sec 208 | skills/image/references/characters.md | ENTRA
Key phrase: "Keep facial features exactly the same as Image 1"

### INV-002058 | sec 209 | skills/image/references/characters.md | ENTRA
## Viral Thumbnails

### INV-002059 | sec 209 | skills/image/references/characters.md | ENTRA
```
Design viral thumbnail using person from Image 1.

Face: Keep features same, change expression to [excited/surprised/shocked]
Action: Pose on [left/right], pointing toward [OBJECT]
Subject: [OBJECT] on opposite side
Graphics: Bold [COLOR] arrow connecting person to object
Text: "[HEADLINE]" in massive pop-style, white outline, drop shadow
Background: Blurred [SETTING]

High saturation and contrast.
```

### INV-002060 | sec 210 | skills/image/references/characters.md | ENTRA
## Multi-Character Consistency

### INV-002061 | sec 210 | skills/image/references/characters.md | ENTRA
```
Create [NUMBER]-part story with these [N] characters.

Identity: Keep attire and features consistent throughout.
Variation: Different angles, expressions, distances per image.
Rule: Only one of each character per image.

Story arc: [DESCRIPTION with emotional highs/lows]
```

### INV-002062 | sec 211 | skills/image/references/characters.md | ENTRA
## Brand Asset Generation

### INV-002063 | sec 211 | skills/image/references/characters.md | ENTRA

```
Using this [PRODUCT] as reference:
Create [NUMBER] [TYPE] shots.

Maintain: Brand consistency, quality level
Vary: Angles, lighting, context
Style: [editorial / lifestyle / product]

Generate one at a time.
```

## Camera & Hardware

### INV-002103 | sec 218 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA
Specify camera type to change the visual DNA of the image.

### INV-002105 | sec 218 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA

### INV-002106 | sec 218 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA

### INV-002107 | sec 218 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA

### INV-002108 | sec 218 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA

### INV-002109 | sec 218 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA
- iPhone — casual, smartphone aesthetic

### INV-002110 | sec 218 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA
- Polaroid — instant film, white border, faded colors

### INV-002120 | sec 218 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA
**Focus:**

### INV-002121 | sec 218 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA
- "Shallow depth of field" — blurred background

### INV-002122 | sec 218 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA
- "Deep focus" — everything sharp

### INV-002123 | sec 218 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA

### INV-002124 | sec 218 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA
- "Tilt-shift" — miniature effect

### INV-002125 | sec 218 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA | lente: «50mm, f/2.8 menos fiable que lo descriptivo» es la regla de Nano Banana (image/SKILL.md:114); en GPT Image 2 se admite «50mm feel» como look (prompt-framework.md:42)
> Note: Named lens parameters (50mm, f/2.8) are less reliable than
> descriptive terms. Prefer "shallow depth of field" over "f/1.8".

## Creative Prompt Examples

### INV-002155 | sec 221 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA
**Cartoon Portrait (3D + Real):**

### INV-002156 | sec 221 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA
```
Based on the uploaded reference image, create a photorealistic scene
featuring the real human standing next to a giant 3D animation-style
version of themselves. Both must have identical facial structures,
clothing, and poses. Clean gray-blue studio background, cinematic lighting.
```

### INV-002157 | sec 221 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA

### INV-002158 | sec 221 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA
```
Convert this uploaded animated still into an ultra-realistic, cinematic
scene. Transform the animated characters into real humans while preserving
their original identities, facial structures, outfits, and expressions.
```

### INV-002159 | sec 221 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA
**Historical Maps (Google Street View style):**

### INV-002160 | sec 221 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA
```
Generate a hyper-realistic image of [HISTORICAL EVENT], perfectly
replicating a Google Maps Street View capture. Include wide-angle barrel
distortion, semi-transparent Maps UI overlay (compass, chevron arrows),
and '© Google [YEAR]' watermark. Automatically blur faces for privacy.
```

### INV-002161 | sec 221 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA
**Kindergarten Filter:**

### INV-002162 | sec 221 | skills/image/references/creative-direction.md | ENTRA
```
A child's crayon drawing on white lined notebook paper of [SUBJECT].
Use chunky wax-crayon strokes, wobbly outlines, bold colors that overflow
the lines. Visible pressure marks, waxy smudges, uneven scribble shading.
Joyful, naive art style.
```

```
Style: [Modern minimalist / Scandinavian / Industrial]
Materials: [flooring], [walls], [textiles]
Lighting: Soft natural light, large windows
Quality: Photorealistic rendering
```

### INV-002196 | sec 230 | skills/image/references/editing.md | SALE | edición en Nano Banana
- **Nano Banana:** conversational, без масок. «Keep X same, change Y». Хорошо понимает физику и материалы.

### INV-002197 | sec 230 | skills/image/references/editing.md | ENTRA
- **GPT Image 2:** двухколоночная логика **Change / Preserve / Constraints**. Preserve list повторять каждую итерацию, иначе drift. Опционально `mask_image_url` для точечных edit'ов. Лучшая identity preservation. См. [gpt-image.md](gpt-image.md#editing--двухколоночная-логика).

### INV-002227 | sec 238 | skills/image/references/editing.md | SALE | «NBP understands materials»
NBP understands materials:

### INV-002239 | sec 240 | skills/image/references/golden-rules.md | ENTRA
Универсальные принципы. Работают для **обеих** семей моделей (Nano Banana и GPT Image 2).
Для модельной специфики см. [nano-banana.md](nano-banana.md), [gpt-image.md](gpt-image.md).

### INV-002274 | sec 248 | skills/image/references/golden-rules.md | ENTRA
> **Thinking Mode** (NB only), **`quality: low/medium/high`** (GPT Image 2 only) — см. соответствующие references.

### INV-002276 | sec 249 | skills/image/references/golden-rules.md | SALE | recorrido de costo en Nano Banana
- **Nano Banana:** прогон вариантов на `0.5K` Flash → отбор → переген победителя на `2K`/`4K`.

### INV-002277 | sec 249 | skills/image/references/golden-rules.md | ENTRA
- **GPT Image 2:** прогон на `quality: low` → отбор → переген на `medium` или `high`.

### INV-002286 | sec 251 | skills/image/references/golden-rules.md | ENTRA
Multi-image вход: **NB до 14**, **GPT Image 2 до 16**. Индексируй с ролью каждой картинки.

### INV-002299 | sec 252 | skills/image/references/golden-rules.md | ENTRA
GPT Image 2 обладает глубокими знаниями о культуре, эпохах и визуальных стилях. Вместо описания каждой детали — дай модели культурный/временной/жанровый якорь, и она сама заполнит аутентичные детали.

### INV-002318 | sec 254 | skills/image/references/golden-rules.md | ENTRA
4. **Era/cultural anchors работают лучше с GPT Image 2** (world knowledge). С Nano Banana результат менее предсказуем — NB больше опирается на явные описания

### INV-002331 | sec 256 | skills/image/references/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
# GPT Image 2 — Specific Rules

### INV-002332 | sec 256 | skills/image/references/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
Production default OpenAI: `gpt-image-2`. Migration-only: `gpt-image-1.5`, `gpt-image-1`. Бюджетный: `gpt-image-1-mini`.

### INV-002334 | sec 257 | skills/image/references/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
GPT Image 2 сильнее всего реагирует на разделение по секциям. Пиши лейблами, не сплошным текстом.

### INV-002340 | sec 259 | skills/image/references/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
GPT Image 2 особенно чувствителен к качеству формулировок. Vague praise = деградация результата.

### INV-002354 | sec 261 | skills/image/references/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
## Размеры (gpt-image-2)

### INV-002365 | sec 261 | skills/image/references/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
> Экстрим вроде 1:8 / 8:1 GPT Image 2 НЕ умеет — иди в Nano Banana.

### INV-002374 | sec 263 | skills/image/references/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
Endpoint: `openai/gpt-image-2/edit` (на fal.ai) или соответствующий через OpenAI API.

### INV-002383 | sec 264 | skills/image/references/gpt-image.md | SALE | input_fidelity: solo gpt-image-1.5/1; el propio texto dice que en gpt-image-2 ya es alto por defecto
**Object removal:** «Remove [X]. Do not change anything else. Use `input_fidelity: high` to maintain surrounding context» (только gpt-image-1.5/1, в gpt-image-2 high-fidelity по умолчанию).

### INV-002392 | sec 267 | skills/image/references/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
GPT Image 2 умеет домысливать контекст: «Bethel, NY, August 1969» → выведет Woodstock-эстетику. Используй: дай исторический/культурный анкер, не расписывай каждую деталь.

### INV-002473 | sec 284 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
# Multi-Panel Compositions

### INV-002474 | sec 284 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
Single-image layouts containing multiple frames, panels, or grid cells. Unlike [storyboards.md](storyboards.md) (sequential images generated one-at-a-time), these patterns produce **one image** with all panels baked in.

### INV-002475 | sec 284 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
Core principle: explicitly number and describe every panel. Models need panel-by-panel instructions — vague requests like "show multiple angles" produce inconsistent grids.

### INV-002476 | sec 284 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
---

### INV-002477 | sec 285 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
## 1. 9-Cell Grid Storyboard

### INV-002478 | sec 285 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
> For a product-focused narrative variant, see [patterns/ecommerce.md](patterns/ecommerce.md#9-panel-tvc-storyboard-grid).

### INV-002479 | sec 285 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**When to use:** TVC or commercial shot breakdown — one image = 9 panels with scene titles and timestamps.

### INV-002480 | sec 286 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
### Template (5-slot format)

### INV-002481 | sec 286 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
```
Scene: Dark storyboard layout — 3x3 grid of cinematic frames for a {duration}-second {product_name} commercial. Each cell has a thin dark border. Below each cell: scene number, title, and timestamp in small white sans-serif text on dark background.

Subject: {product_name} commercial storyboard showing the complete narrative arc across 9 panels.

Important Details:
Panel 1 (0:00–{t1}): {scene_1_description}
Panel 2 ({t1}–{t2}): {scene_2_description}
Panel 3 ({t2}–{t3}): {scene_3_description}
Panel 4 ({t3}–{t4}): {scene_4_description}
Panel 5 ({t4}–{t5}): {scene_5_description}
Panel 6 ({t5}–{t6}): {scene_6_description}
Panel 7 ({t6}–{t7}): {scene_7_description}
Panel 8 ({t7}–{t8}): {scene_8_description}
Panel 9 ({t8}–{duration}): {scene_9_description}

Each panel labeled below: "Scene {N}: {title}" and "{start}–{end}"

Use Case: Pre-production storyboard for video production team
Constraints: All 9 panels must be clearly separated, no merged cells, every panel must contain distinct content, timestamps must be legible, {language} titles
```

### INV-002482 | sec 286 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Variables:**

### INV-002483 | sec 286 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{product_name}` — brand/product

### INV-002484 | sec 286 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{duration}` — total spot length (e.g., "30", "60")

### INV-002485 | sec 286 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{t1}` through `{t8}` — timestamp boundaries

### INV-002486 | sec 286 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{scene_1_description}` through `{scene_9_description}` — shot description per panel

### INV-002487 | sec 286 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{language}` — "Chinese" / "English" / "bilingual"

### INV-002488 | sec 286 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Recommended size:** 1536x1024 (landscape)
**Model:** GPT Image 2 `quality: high` (text-heavy — timestamps and titles need legibility)
**Common pitfalls:**

### INV-002489 | sec 286 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Omitting panel numbers causes the model to merge or skip panels

### INV-002490 | sec 286 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Vague scene descriptions produce near-identical panels — each must have a distinct action, angle, or subject

### INV-002491 | sec 286 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Timestamps in very small text need `quality: high` to remain readable

### INV-002492 | sec 286 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA

### INV-002493 | sec 287 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
## 2. 2x2 Editorial Portrait Grid

### INV-002494 | sec 287 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**When to use:** Same person shown from 4 angles/crops in one image for an editorial or casting look.

### INV-002495 | sec 288 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
### Template (5-slot format)

### INV-002496 | sec 288 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
```
Scene: 2x2 grid of four editorial portraits, minimal gap between panels. {background_description}. {lighting_style}.

Subject: {subject_description} — same person in all four panels, consistent identity and wardrobe.

Important Details:
Top-left: front-facing portrait, direct eye contact, shoulders up
Top-right: extreme macro close-up of face — eyes, skin texture, freckles visible
Bottom-left: lower angle looking up, chin slightly raised, confident expression
Bottom-right: side profile, clean silhouette against background

Consistent lighting across all four panels. Skin texture realistic, no airbrushing.

Use Case: editorial photography, model portfolio, casting composite
Constraints: same person in every panel, no wardrobe changes between panels, no extra people, no text overlays
```

### INV-002497 | sec 288 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Variables:**

### INV-002498 | sec 288 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{subject_description}` — age, appearance, wardrobe (e.g., "woman in her 30s, dark curly hair, white linen shirt")

### INV-002499 | sec 288 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{background_description}` — (e.g., "soft gray studio backdrop")

### INV-002500 | sec 288 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{lighting_style}` — (e.g., "single key light from upper left, subtle fill from right")

### INV-002501 | sec 288 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Recommended size:** 1024x1024 (square) or 1024x1536 (vertical for portrait emphasis)
**Model:** GPT Image 2 `quality: medium` or Nano Banana Pro (both handle photorealistic portraits well)
**Common pitfalls:**

### INV-002502 | sec 288 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Not specifying "same person" in every panel — model may generate four different people

### INV-002503 | sec 288 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Omitting which quadrant gets which angle — model arranges arbitrarily

### INV-002504 | sec 288 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Requesting too-different crops (full body + extreme close-up) creates scale inconsistency within the grid

### INV-002505 | sec 288 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA

## 3. 3-Panel Campaign Collage

### INV-002507 | sec 289 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**When to use:** Hero shot + close-up + action for a campaign visual — horizontal or vertical triptych.

### INV-002508 | sec 290 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
### Template (5-slot format)

### INV-002509 | sec 290 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
```
Scene: Three-panel {orientation} collage for {brand_name} campaign. Panels separated by thin white lines.

Subject: {model_description} showcasing {product_description}.

Important Details:
Panel 1 (left/top): Hero wide shot — full figure of model with product, environmental context, lifestyle setting
Panel 2 (center/middle): Close-up product detail — {product_detail_description}, tactile textures, sharp focus
Panel 3 (right/bottom): Action shot — model using/wearing/interacting with product, candid energy, slight motion blur on extremities

Consistent warm golden-hour lighting across all panels. Same model identity throughout.
{typography_instruction}

Use Case: social media campaign visual, brand lookbook
Constraints: same person across all panels, consistent color grading, no stock-photo stiffness, {product_name} must be visible in every panel
```

### INV-002510 | sec 290 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Variables:**

### INV-002511 | sec 290 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{orientation}` — "horizontal" (side by side) or "vertical" (stacked)

### INV-002512 | sec 290 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{brand_name}`, `{product_name}`, `{product_description}` — brand context

### INV-002513 | sec 290 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{model_description}` — who appears

### INV-002514 | sec 290 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{product_detail_description}` — what the close-up shows

### INV-002515 | sec 290 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{typography_instruction}` — e.g., `Text: "{HEADLINE}" in bold condensed white, overlaid on Panel 1 lower third` or omit for no text

### INV-002516 | sec 290 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Recommended size:** 1536x1024 (horizontal triptych) or 1024x1536 (vertical triptych)
**Model:** GPT Image 2 `quality: medium` — if text overlay needed, use `quality: high`
**Common pitfalls:**

### INV-002517 | sec 290 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Not specifying panel order — "hero, close-up, action" without left/center/right assignment

### INV-002518 | sec 290 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Lighting inconsistency when mixing indoor close-up with outdoor hero shot — specify unified lighting

### INV-002519 | sec 290 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Forgetting to mention subject consistency causes three different models

### INV-002520 | sec 290 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA

### INV-002521 | sec 291 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
## 4. 4x3 Borderless Grid

### INV-002522 | sec 291 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**When to use:** 12 panels telling a story or showing moods — no gaps between panels, seamless mosaic feel.

### INV-002523 | sec 292 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
### Template (5-slot format)

### INV-002524 | sec 292 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
```
Scene: 4x3 borderless grid (4 columns, 3 rows) where each of the 12 panels is an independent image but panels share no borders, gaps, or dividers — edges touch seamlessly. Overall theme: {theme}.

Subject: {subject_description} — maintain strong subject consistency across all 12 panels.

Important Details:
Row 1: {panel_1}, {panel_2}, {panel_3}, {panel_4}
Row 2: {panel_5}, {panel_6}, {panel_7}, {panel_8}
Row 3: {panel_9}, {panel_10}, {panel_11}, {panel_12}

Style: {style_description}
Mood progression: {mood_arc}
Each panel is an independent composition but the overall grid reads as a unified artwork.

Use Case: mood board, editorial spread, social media carousel preview, album artwork
Constraints: borderless — no white lines, no black borders, no gaps between panels. Same subject identity across all panels. No text in panels.
```

### INV-002525 | sec 292 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Variables:**

### INV-002526 | sec 292 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{subject_description}` — who/what appears across panels

### INV-002527 | sec 292 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{theme}` — overarching concept (e.g., "solitude in a city", "four seasons of a garden")

### INV-002528 | sec 292 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{panel_1}` through `{panel_12}` — brief shot description per cell

### INV-002529 | sec 292 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{style_description}` — visual style (e.g., "35mm film grain, desaturated palette")

### INV-002530 | sec 292 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{mood_arc}` — how energy changes across the grid (e.g., "calm morning to chaotic night")

### INV-002531 | sec 292 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | SALE | Borderless Grid: modelo NBP
**Recommended size:** 1536x1024 (landscape) — gives each cell enough resolution
**Model:** Nano Banana Pro (handles complex multi-element compositions with thinking mode; no text needed)
**Common pitfalls:**

### INV-002532 | sec 292 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Saying "borderless" is not enough — explicitly state "no white lines, no black borders, no gaps"

### INV-002533 | sec 292 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- 12 panels in one image pushes detail limits — keep per-panel descriptions short and visually distinct

### INV-002534 | sec 292 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Without explicit subject consistency instructions, each panel may feature a different person/object

### INV-002535 | sec 292 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA

### INV-002536 | sec 293 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
## 5. 6-Frame Cinematic Sequence

### INV-002537 | sec 293 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**When to use:** Fashion editorial or film-style sequence — multiple camera angles of the same scene in one image.

### INV-002538 | sec 294 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
### Template (5-slot format)

### INV-002539 | sec 294 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
```
Scene: 6-frame cinematic sequence arranged in a 3x2 grid. Dark film-strip aesthetic with thin black borders. {location_description}.

Subject: {subject_description}, wearing {wardrobe_description}. Same person, same outfit, same location across all 6 frames.

Important Details:
Frame 1 (top-left): Top-down bird's eye view — subject seen from directly above, full body visible against ground/floor
Frame 2 (top-center): Low angle looking up — subject towering over camera, dramatic perspective, sky/ceiling visible
Frame 3 (top-right): Wide isolation shot — subject small in frame, vast environment dominates, sense of scale
Frame 4 (bottom-left): Close-up with slight tilt — face and upper body, Dutch angle, intimate intensity
Frame 5 (bottom-center): Motion frame — subject mid-action ({motion_action}), natural motion blur on limbs
Frame 6 (bottom-right): Grounded final — medium shot, subject at rest, direct gaze, resolving the sequence

Photographer reference: {photographer_style}
Lighting: consistent {lighting_description} across all frames

Use Case: fashion editorial, film lookbook, director's shot list visualization
Constraints: same person and wardrobe in every frame, no costume changes, consistent color grading, no text overlays
```

### INV-002540 | sec 294 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Variables:**

### INV-002541 | sec 294 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{subject_description}` — model details

### INV-002542 | sec 294 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{wardrobe_description}` — clothing

### INV-002543 | sec 294 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{location_description}` — setting

### INV-002544 | sec 294 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{motion_action}` — what the motion frame captures (e.g., "walking forward", "turning sharply", "jumping")

### INV-002545 | sec 294 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{photographer_style}` — (e.g., "Peter Lindbergh desaturated realism", "Helmut Newton dramatic contrast")

### INV-002546 | sec 294 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{lighting_description}` — (e.g., "overcast natural light, soft shadows")

### INV-002547 | sec 294 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Recommended size:** 1536x1024 (landscape, 3x2 grid)
**Model:** GPT Image 2 `quality: medium` or Nano Banana Pro
**Common pitfalls:**

### INV-002548 | sec 294 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Not naming each frame explicitly — "various angles" is too vague, the model needs per-frame instructions

### INV-002549 | sec 294 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Top-down and low-angle in the same grid confuse the model if you don't anchor each frame to a grid position

### INV-002550 | sec 294 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Motion blur instruction must be specific ("blur on hands and feet") or the model applies blur everywhere

## 6. Before/After Split

### INV-002553 | sec 295 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**When to use:** Product transformation, makeover, time comparison, renovation — two states side by side.

### INV-002554 | sec 296 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA

### INV-002555 | sec 296 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
```
Scene: Single image split into left and right halves with a {divider_style} dividing line down the center. Before/after comparison.

Subject: {subject_description}

Important Details:
Left half (BEFORE): {before_description} — {before_condition}
Right half (AFTER): {after_description} — {after_condition}

The transition at the center line should feel {transition_style}. Same camera angle, same framing, same background perspective in both halves — only the subject's state changes.

Use Case: {use_case}
Constraints: identical composition and camera angle on both sides, same lighting direction, no text unless specified, the dividing line must be clearly visible
```

### INV-002556 | sec 296 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA

### INV-002557 | sec 296 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA

### INV-002558 | sec 296 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{before_description}` / `{before_condition}` — left side state (e.g., "faded, cracked wall with peeling paint")

### INV-002559 | sec 296 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{after_description}` / `{after_condition}` — right side state (e.g., "freshly painted wall, smooth finish, vibrant color")

### INV-002560 | sec 296 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{divider_style}` — "thin white line" / "subtle gradient blend" / "sharp vertical cut"

### INV-002561 | sec 296 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{transition_style}` — "clean and abrupt" / "natural, as if wiping away"

### INV-002562 | sec 296 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{use_case}` — "product marketing", "renovation portfolio", "skincare results"

### INV-002563 | sec 296 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Recommended size:** 1536x1024 (landscape — gives each half a portrait-like proportion)
**Model:** GPT Image 2 `quality: medium` — for text labels ("BEFORE" / "AFTER"), use `quality: high`
**Common pitfalls:**

### INV-002564 | sec 296 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Not specifying "same camera angle both sides" — model may show two completely different viewpoints

### INV-002565 | sec 296 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Without a visible divider, the two halves can merge into one ambiguous scene

### INV-002566 | sec 296 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA

### INV-002568 | sec 297 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
## 7. 12-Panel Storyboard Poster

### INV-002569 | sec 297 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**When to use:** Full narrative in one image — 3x4 grid (3 columns, 4 rows) for animation or video pre-production.

### INV-002570 | sec 298 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
### Template (5-slot format)

### INV-002571 | sec 298 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
```
Scene: 12-panel storyboard poster, 3 columns x 4 rows. Dark background with each panel in a clean rectangular frame. "{title}" in bold white text at the top of the image. Below each panel: scene number and one-line description in small white text.

Subject: {character_description} — maintain consistent character design, proportions, and colors across all 12 panels.

Important Details:
Panel 1: {scene_1} — "{caption_1}"
Panel 2: {scene_2} — "{caption_2}"
Panel 3: {scene_3} — "{caption_3}"
Panel 4: {scene_4} — "{caption_4}"
Panel 5: {scene_5} — "{caption_5}"
Panel 6: {scene_6} — "{caption_6}"
Panel 7: {scene_7} — "{caption_7}"
Panel 8: {scene_8} — "{caption_8}"
Panel 9: {scene_9} — "{caption_9}"
Panel 10: {scene_10} — "{caption_10}"
Panel 11: {scene_11} — "{caption_11}"
Panel 12: {scene_12} — "{caption_12}"

Panels read left-to-right, top-to-bottom (like a comic page). Character appearance, clothing, and color palette must stay identical across all 12 panels — only pose, expression, angle, and environment change.

Style: {art_style}

Use Case: animation pre-production, pitch deck visualization, narrative overview poster
Constraints: all 12 panels must be distinct scenes (no duplicates), character consistency is critical, scene numbers must be legible, {language} captions
Quality: high
```

### INV-002572 | sec 298 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Variables:**

### INV-002573 | sec 298 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{title}` — project/episode title displayed at top

### INV-002574 | sec 298 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{character_description}` — detailed character design (colors, outfit, distinguishing features)

### INV-002575 | sec 298 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{scene_1}` through `{scene_12}` — visual description of each panel

### INV-002576 | sec 298 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{caption_1}` through `{caption_12}` — text label under each panel

### INV-002577 | sec 298 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{art_style}` — (e.g., "Pixar-style 3D", "anime cel shading", "watercolor illustration", "graphic novel ink")

### INV-002578 | sec 298 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- `{language}` — caption language

### INV-002579 | sec 298 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Recommended size:** 1024x1536 (portrait — 3 columns x 4 rows needs vertical space)
**Model:** GPT Image 2 `quality: high` (text-heavy — scene numbers and captions must be readable)
**Common pitfalls:**

### INV-002580 | sec 298 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Character drift is the biggest risk at 12 panels — repeat character design details in the prompt, not just "same character"

### INV-002581 | sec 298 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Without explicit scene numbering in the prompt, panels may appear in random order

### INV-002582 | sec 298 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- 12 panels with captions is extremely text-dense — keep captions under 5 words each for legibility

### INV-002583 | sec 298 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Art style must be stated once and applied uniformly; mixing styles across panels produces visual chaos

### INV-002584 | sec 298 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA

### INV-002585 | sec 299 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
## General Multi-Panel Tips

### INV-002586 | sec 299 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Panel count vs. detail trade-off:** More panels = less detail per panel. 4 panels allow rich per-panel descriptions; 12 panels need brief, visually distinct descriptions (3-8 words each).

### INV-002587 | sec 299 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Subject consistency:** Always include an explicit instruction: "same person / same character / same product across all panels." Repeat key identity markers (hair color, clothing, distinguishing features) rather than saying "same as before."

### INV-002588 | sec 299 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Grid specification:** Always state the grid dimensions (e.g., "3x2 grid, 3 columns 2 rows"). Saying "6 panels" without layout instruction lets the model choose an unpredictable arrangement.

### INV-002589 | sec 299 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Borders and gaps:** Be explicit — "thin white border between panels" or "borderless, no gaps." Default behavior varies by model and is unreliable.

### INV-002590 | sec 299 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Reading order:** State it: "left-to-right, top-to-bottom" or "numbered 1-9 starting top-left." Without this, narrative flow may be jumbled.

### INV-002591 | sec 299 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
**Model selection summary:**

### INV-002592 | sec 299 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Text/labels in panels --> GPT Image 2 `quality: high`

### INV-002593 | sec 299 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | SALE | sin texto y composición compleja: NBP
- No text, complex composition --> Nano Banana Pro

### INV-002594 | sec 299 | skills/image/references/multi-panel.md | ENTRA
- Budget/exploration --> Nano Banana 2 or GPT Image 2 `quality: low`

### INV-003020 | sec 369 | skills/image/references/prompt-framework.md | ENTRA
> ⚠️ **Параметры объектива** (50mm, 85mm, f/2.8, ISO):
> - **Nano Banana** — игнорит числа, пиши описательно («shallow depth of field»)
> - **GPT Image 2** — допускает «50mm feel» как high-level look, но не как точную физическую симуляцию

### INV-003035 | sec 371 | skills/image/references/prompt-framework.md | ENTRA | el imperativo «formula en positivo» es genérico; NBP es el ejemplo
> Формулируй позитивно! NBP лучше понимает "clean background" чем "no clutter"

### INV-003039 | sec 372 | skills/image/references/prompt-framework.md | ENTRA | dice qué solo hace Nano Banana, es decir, qué no pedirle a GPT Image 2
> Image grounding (поиск реальных мест в интернете) и экстремальные ratio (1:8, 8:1, 4:1) — только Nano Banana. См. [nano-banana.md](nano-banana.md).

### INV-003088 | sec 387 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
# Presentation Slides

### INV-003089 | sec 387 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
Professional slide visuals for decks and pitches.

### INV-003090 | sec 388 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
## Design Principles

### INV-003091 | sec 389 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### One Slide = One Idea

### INV-003092 | sec 389 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
Не комбинируй несвязные концепции. Аудитория запоминает 65% визуального vs 10% услышанного.

### INV-003093 | sec 390 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Content Limits

### INV-003094 | sec 390 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Max 5 пунктов на слайде

### INV-003095 | sec 390 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- 15-20 слов в блоке текста

### INV-003096 | sec 390 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Заголовок + список ИЛИ абзац, не оба

### INV-003097 | sec 391 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Typography

### INV-003098 | sec 391 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
```
Заголовок: 44-54 pt, bold
Подзаголовок: 28-36 pt, medium
Текст: 18-24 pt, regular (min 18 для читаемости)

Шрифты: sans-serif основной (Inter, Helvetica, Roboto)
Serif только для акцента
```

### INV-003099 | sec 392 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Contrast & Color

### INV-003100 | sec 392 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- 3-4 цвета max (основной + 2-3 акцента)

### INV-003101 | sec 392 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Один нейтральный фон (white/black/gray)

### INV-003102 | sec 392 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Контраст min 4.5:1

### INV-003103 | sec 392 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Избегай pure white #fff на чёрном - используй #f5f5f5

### INV-003104 | sec 393 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Whitespace

### INV-003105 | sec 393 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Контент должен дышать

### INV-003106 | sec 393 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Асимметричные макеты (2/3 + 1/3) динамичнее центрированных

### INV-003107 | sec 393 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Если тесно - разбей на два слайда

### INV-003108 | sec 394 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Visual Consistency

### INV-003109 | sec 394 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Один стиль: фото ИЛИ иллюстрации ИЛИ минимал графика

### INV-003110 | sec 394 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Смешивание = визуальный хаос

### INV-003111 | sec 394 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- High-res only, каждый образ усиливает идею

### INV-003112 | sec 395 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Checklist

### INV-003113 | sec 395 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
```
☐ Один слайд = одна идея
☐ Max 5 пунктов
☐ Заголовок 44-54pt, текст 18-24pt
☐ 3-4 цвета, высокий контраст
☐ Достаточно whitespace
☐ High-res визуалы, единый стиль
```

### INV-003114 | sec 395 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
---

### INV-003115 | sec 396 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
## Slide Types

### INV-003116 | sec 397 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Hero Title (Dark)

### INV-003117 | sec 397 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
```
Create dramatic title slide for [CONTEXT] presentation.

Background: Gradient from [DARK hex] to #0a0a0a.
Atmospheric: subtle mist at lower edge.

Typography:
"[MAIN TITLE]" in extra bold, white, extremely large, upper third
"[SUBTITLE]" in thin, muted, 5x smaller, below

Mood: Confident, premium
Format: 16:9
```

### INV-003118 | sec 398 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Data Visualization

### INV-003119 | sec 398 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
```
Create data slide for [CONTEXT] presentation.

Background: Dark gradient [hex] to [hex].

Hero number: "[METRIC]" in bold, [ACCENT], center
Trend: [up/down arrow] in [green/red]
Supporting: 3-4 smaller metrics below

Chart: [TYPE] showing [DATA]

Mood: Analytical, impactful
Format: 16:9
```

### INV-003120 | sec 399 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Comparison (Light)

### INV-003121 | sec 399 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
```
Create comparison slide for [CONTEXT].

Background: Off-white #fafafa to #f0efed.

Split layout:
Left: "[OPTION A]" with details
Right: "[OPTION B]" with details

Emphasis: Right side accent bar #3b82f6

Mood: Clear, objective
Format: 16:9
```

### INV-003122 | sec 400 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Insight/Problem

### INV-003123 | sec 400 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
```
Create insight slide for [CONTEXT].

Background: Clean off-white.

Focal: "[KEY MESSAGE]" in bold, centered
Supporting: "[CONTEXT]" smaller, below
Emphasis bar: [ACCENT] strip with data point

Mood: Clarity, focus
Format: 16:9
```

### INV-003124 | sec 401 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Process/Timeline

### INV-003125 | sec 401 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
```
Create process slide for [CONTEXT].

Flow: [HORIZONTAL/VERTICAL]
Stages: [1]→[2]→[3]→[4]
Each: icon + "[LABEL]" + description
Connectors: arrows in [ACCENT]

Format: 16:9
```

### INV-003152 | sec 405 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
## Layout Systems

### INV-003153 | sec 405 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
Универсальные правила вёрстки. Работают с любым визуальным стилем.

### INV-003154 | sec 405 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
---

### INV-003155 | sec 406 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Bento Grid

### INV-003156 | sec 406 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
Модульная система из блоков разного размера.

### INV-003157 | sec 406 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
**Core Rules:**

### INV-003158 | sec 406 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Родительский контейнер с явными границами модулей

### INV-003159 | sec 406 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Иерархия: важный контент → крупные блоки, второстепенный → мелкие

### INV-003160 | sec 406 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Max 9 блоков (иначе перегруз)

### INV-003161 | sec 406 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Единообразные gaps (16px стандарт)

### INV-003162 | sec 406 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Связанный контент группируется в одном блоке

### INV-003163 | sec 406 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
**Размеры:** широкие/плоские (hero) | узкие/высокие (списки) | квадратные (иконки)

### INV-003164 | sec 406 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
**Сетки:**

### INV-003165 | sec 406 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
```
3-block:           6-block:             9-block:
┌───────┬───┐     ┌───┬───┬───┐       ┌─────┬───┬───┐
│ HERO  │SEC│     │ S │ S │ S │       │HERO │MED│ S │
├───────┴───┤     ├───┴───┼───┤       ├──┬──┴───┼───┤
│  FOOTER   │     │ CHART │LST│       │S │ MED  │ S │
└───────────┘     └───────┴───┘       └──┴──────┴───┘
```

### INV-003166 | sec 406 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
**Prompt:**

### INV-003167 | sec 406 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
```
Layout: bento grid, [N] blocks
Hero block: [MAIN], Medium: [SECONDARY], Small: [ICONS]
Gaps: uniform spacing
```

### INV-003168 | sec 406 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
---

### INV-003169 | sec 407 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Asymmetric Grid

### INV-003170 | sec 407 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
Динамичные композиции с визуальным балансом.

### INV-003171 | sec 407 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
**Rules:**

### INV-003172 | sec 407 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Начинай с базовой сетки, потом ломай осознанно

### INV-003173 | sec 407 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Балансируй вес: крупный элемент слева = несколько мелких справа

### INV-003174 | sec 407 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Rule of Thirds для естественного баланса

### INV-003175 | sec 407 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Ключевые элементы выделяй размером и положением

### INV-003176 | sec 407 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
**Применять:** креативные презентации, портфолио, fashion, стартапы
**НЕ применять:** банки, госпорталы, B2B с большими данными, пожилая аудитория

### INV-003177 | sec 407 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
**Prompt:**

### INV-003178 | sec 407 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
```
Layout: asymmetric composition
Left: [LARGE - 60%], Right: [2-3 SMALLER stacked]
Balance: visual weight distributed
Rule of thirds positioning
```

### INV-003179 | sec 407 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
---

### INV-003180 | sec 408 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Negative Space

### INV-003181 | sec 408 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
Пространство как инструмент фокусировки.

### INV-003182 | sec 408 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
**Rules:**

### INV-003183 | sec 408 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Структурирует, а не создаёт пустоту ради красоты

### INV-003184 | sec 408 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Управляет вниманием, фокусирует на главном

### INV-003185 | sec 408 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
- Gestalt: элементы с пространством = отдельные, близко = связанные

### INV-003186 | sec 408 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
**Применять:** премиальные бренды, минималистичные презентации, hero-секции
**НЕ применять:** новостные порталы, дашборды, мобильные версии

### INV-003187 | sec 408 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
**Prompt:**

### INV-003188 | sec 408 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
```
Layout: generous whitespace
Focal point: [ELEMENT] with breathing room
Empty space: intentional, guides eye to [CTA]
```

## Futuristic / SaaS Style

### INV-003210 | sec 411 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
Apple Keynote minimalism + glassmorphism + 3D objects.

### INV-003211 | sec 412 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Visual Language

### INV-003212 | sec 412 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
```
Style: Apple Keynote + SaaS + glassmorphism
Mood: premium, immersive, clean, breathable
Lighting: volumetric, ray-traced reflections, ambient occlusion
Base: deep void black OR pure ceramic white
Accents: aurora gradients (neon purple, electric blue, coral, cyan)
```

### INV-003213 | sec 413 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Glassmorphism Cards

### INV-003214 | sec 413 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
```
Material: frosted glass with blur
Edges: delicate white borders
Shadow: soft, diffused
Spacing: generous internal whitespace
```

### INV-003215 | sec 414 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### 3D Visual Anchors

### INV-003216 | sec 414 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
```
Purpose: abstract 3D artifacts as focal points
Materials: polished metal, iridescent acrylic, transparent glass, soft silicone
Shapes: capsules, spheres, shields, Möbius strips, fluid waves
Quality: looks like expensive collectibles
```

### INV-003217 | sec 415 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
### Composition by Type

### INV-003218 | sec 415 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA
**Cover:** huge 3D glass object center + bold title + aurora background
**Content:** bento grid + 3D icons in small cards + text in large cards  
**Data:** split-screen (text left, glowing 3D chart right)

### INV-003219 | sec 416 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA

### INV-003220 | sec 416 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA

```
3D donut charts, glowing
Capsule-shaped progress bars
Floating numbers with neon glow
Style: looks like glowing neon toys
```

### INV-003221 | sec 417 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA

### INV-003222 | sec 417 | skills/image/references/slides.md | ENTRA

```
Create a futuristic SaaS slide for product presentation.

Style: Apple Keynote + glassmorphism.
Mood: premium, immersive, clean.
Background: void black with aurora gradient (purple → cyan).

Layout: bento grid, 6 blocks.
Cards: frosted glass, blur, white edges, soft shadows.
Hero block: floating iridescent sphere.
Data blocks: glowing 3D donut chart, neon metrics.

Typography: clean sans-serif, high contrast white.
Format: 16:9
```

```
Identity: Characters and attire consistent throughout.
Variation: Different angles, distances, expressions.
Generate: One image at a time.
Format: [ASPECT RATIO] per image.
```

### INV-003393 | sec 445 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
# Vision Decomposer — Image-to-Prompt Analysis

### INV-003394 | sec 445 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
Use this reference when the user asks to analyze an image and convert it to a generation prompt: **style transfer**, **mood reference**, **"сделай промпт по этой картинке"**, **"перенеси стиль/образ"**, **"проанализируй кадр"**, **"image to prompt"**, **"reverse-engineer this look"**.

### INV-003395 | sec 445 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
You become a **professional Vision Agent**. Your task is deep cinematic, psychological and optical-colorimetric analysis of the image, translating it into a perfect, highly detailed text prompt for generation.

### INV-003396 | sec 445 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
The process has two strict steps. Do not skip Step 1. Do not freestyle Step 2.

### INV-003397 | sec 445 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
---

### INV-003398 | sec 446 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
## STEP 1 — DEEP DECOMPOSITION

### INV-003399 | sec 446 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
Scan the image as **raw data** and extract facts based on:

### INV-003400 | sec 446 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Cinematic composition** — Bruce Block's visual structure

### INV-003401 | sec 446 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Color theory** — Itten

### INV-003402 | sec 446 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Perceptual psychology** — Arnheim

### INV-003403 | sec 446 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Directing references** — Spielberg / Scorsese / Tarantino

### INV-003404 | sec 446 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Cinematography & colorimetry** — Valentin Zheleznyakov

### INV-003405 | sec 446 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
Evaluate the following four parameter blocks.

### INV-003406 | sec 447 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
### 1. Subject — psychology and mise-en-scène

### INV-003407 | sec 447 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Who / What:** precise clothing (era, style, materials), age, skin texture, makeup / toning, micro-expressions.

### INV-003408 | sec 447 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Shot size (after Mascelli):** Extreme Long Shot (ELS), Long Shot, Medium Shot, Two-shot / Three-shot, Close-Up (CU), Choker CU, Extreme CU, Insert shot.

### INV-003409 | sec 447 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Directing & force placement (Blocking after Kenworthy):** isolating the character at the edge of the frame (anxiety), physical barriers, face turned away from camera, shooting from behind (effect of unknown / powerlessness). Height difference (Level Change) for dominance.

### INV-003410 | sec 447 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Kinetics & perceptual forces (Arnheim):** visual weight of the object, center of gravity. Internal tension of form (compression / stretching / twisting). Pose, plasticity, motion vectors. Imitation of swift action (motion blur) or monumental stillness.

### INV-003411 | sec 447 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Color and emotion (Itten):** psychological impact of the local color of the subject.

### INV-003412 | sec 447 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Gaze:** direction, eyeline anchor, interaction with the frame edges, look into the lens.

### INV-003413 | sec 448 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
### 2. Environment — geometry and structure of space

### INV-003414 | sec 448 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Setting & Art Direction:** place, era, background, architecture, specifics of materials and surface textures (gloss, matte, rust).

### INV-003415 | sec 448 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Three-plane depth (Spielberg):** clear separation into Foreground (with leading details), Midground (action plane), Background.

### INV-003416 | sec 448 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Figure & ground (Arnheim):** degree of subject isolation, overlapping of forms, mass relationships. Use of reflections (mirrors, windows) to expand context.

### INV-003417 | sec 448 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Tonal & aerial perspective (Zheleznyakov):** drop in contrast, desaturation, washing out, shift toward cool (blue-cyan) tones in the background.

### INV-003418 | sec 448 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Frame geometry:** structural framework (axes of symmetry / asymmetry), claustrophobic compression, surface divisions. Illusion of depth via Foreground framing.

### INV-003419 | sec 449 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
### 3. Lighting — chiaroscuro, color and contrast

### INV-003420 | sec 449 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Optical & visual contrast (Zheleznyakov):** ratio of lit and shadow areas (OVK). Depth and density of shadows. Exact local color vs. color modified by lighting (valeurs).

### INV-003421 | sec 449 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Lighting scheme:** Key, Fill, Backlight / Rim, Modeling. Rembrandt lighting, Chiaroscuro. Height, hardness (Hard / Soft) and type of source.

### INV-003422 | sec 449 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Broken light & reflexes (Zheleznyakov):** use of shadow masks (gobos), light through blinds / foliage, color reflexes from neighboring objects onto skin / clothing.

### INV-003423 | sec 449 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Tonality & color:** light key (High-key / Low-key). Threshold Silhouette. Itten's 7 contrasts. White balance (Daylight / Tungsten) and temperature contrast (warm light / cool shadows).

### INV-003424 | sec 450 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
### 4. Tech & Cinematography — optics, filters and film texture

### INV-003425 | sec 450 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Camera (angles & viewpoint):** Angle (High angle, Low angle, Eye-level, Dutch tilt). Objective camera, Subjective, POV, Over-the-shoulder (OTS). Movement imitation (Push-in, Tracking shot).

### INV-003426 | sec 450 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Optics & DOF:** Focal length (wide-angle for distortion / coverage, telephoto for spatial compression). Depth of field. Rack focus, bokeh.

### INV-003427 | sec 450 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Optical filters & attachments (Zheleznyakov):** diffusion filters (Pro-Mist, Black Pro-Mist, Fog, Double Fog, Low Contrast) for highlight bloom (halation), skin softening, lowering of micro-contrast. Polarizers (cutting reflections).

### INV-003428 | sec 450 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Effects & texture:** Frame format (70mm, 35mm, IMAX). Exposure (motion blur). Stylization (Bleach Bypass). Film Grain, chromatic aberration.

### INV-003429 | sec 450 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
---

### INV-003430 | sec 451 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
## STEP 2 — PROMPT SYNTHESIS

### INV-003431 | sec 451 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
Assemble the final prompt from Step 1 data.

### INV-003432 | sec 451 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
Rules:

### INV-003433 | sec 451 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA | «solo keywords separadas por comas» choca con el adaptador GPT Image: «Don't write Midjourney-style comma stacks» (adapters/gpt-image.md:86)
- Write **only keywords separated by commas, in English**.

### INV-003434 | sec 451 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- Describe **strictly what you see**. Do not invent new objects.

### INV-003435 | sec 451 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **No filler.** Never write "The image shows...", "A picture of...", "I can see...".

### INV-003436 | sec 451 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- **Strict word order** (this formula is non-negotiable):

### INV-003437 | sec 451 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
```
[Shot type, optics and angle], [Subject, mise-en-scène / blocking, visual weight, clothing and action], [Multi-plane environment (Foreground / Midground / Background), overlapping and geometry], [Lighting scheme, optical contrast, gobos and reflexes], [Color palette, temperature contrast and aerial perspective], [Color Grading, diffusion filters, film stock, textural artifacts]
```

### INV-003438 | sec 451 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
---

### INV-003439 | sec 452 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
## OUTPUT PROTOCOL

### INV-003440 | sec 452 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
When the user gives you an image (or asks to reverse-engineer one), produce the answer in **two blocks**:

### INV-003441 | sec 453 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
### Block 1 — Brief Analysis Log

### INV-003442 | sec 453 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
```
Subject & Blocking: ...
Environment & Depth: ...
Lighting & Contrast: ...
Tech & Optics: ...
```

### INV-003443 | sec 453 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
Each line: 1-2 sentences max. Just the extracted parameters.

### INV-003444 | sec 454 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
### Block 2 — Final Prompt

### INV-003445 | sec 454 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
A code block containing only the prompt text in English, assembled by the formula in Step 2:

### INV-003446 | sec 454 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
````
```

### INV-003447 | sec 454 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
<comma-separated keywords following the strict 6-segment formula>

### INV-003448 | sec 454 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
```
````

### INV-003449 | sec 454 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
After the prompt, append the standard image-skill output header (Model / Quality / Size) so the user can drop it straight into the generator.

### INV-003450 | sec 454 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA

### INV-003451 | sec 455 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
## When to load this file

### INV-003452 | sec 455 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
Load `vision-decomposer.md` whenever:

### INV-003453 | sec 455 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- The user attaches an image and asks for a prompt to **recreate / transfer / match** its style

### INV-003454 | sec 455 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- The user asks to **decompose, deconstruct, reverse-engineer** a visual reference

### INV-003455 | sec 455 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- The user says **"перенеси стиль", "сделай так же", "повтори образ", "проанализируй кадр", "разбери картинку на промпт"**

### INV-003456 | sec 455 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
- Mood-board work: extracting cinematic DNA from a film still, ad frame, painting, photo

### INV-003457 | sec 455 | skills/image/references/vision-decomposer.md | ENTRA
Do NOT load this for: pure generation requests where no reference image is given. For those, use the standard model files.

### INV-009765 | sec 1093 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
---
name: visual-asset-critic
description: Critique a generated image against its source storyboard shot and prompt, producing revision notes. Use when the user has generated an image and wants feedback before committing. Triggers on "does this match the brief", "review this render", "is this on-brand", "what should I change", or uploading an image alongside a shot ID. The QA loop for AI visuals. Works even without a storyboard-architect run. Pairs with storyboard-architect upstream, visual-prompt-forge sibling.
