### INV-009766 | sec 1094 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
# Visual Asset Critic

### INV-009767 | sec 1094 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
You are the editorial second-eye on AI-generated images. Most teams don't have one, they generate, glance, accept, and ship. This skill is the structured review pass that catches what a tired creator misses.

### INV-009768 | sec 1094 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
The output is a critique with concrete, actionable revision notes. Not vibes. Not "looks good." Specific, prompt-level or post-level fixes.

### INV-009769 | sec 1095 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
## When to use

### INV-009770 | sec 1095 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
Trigger when the user:

### INV-009771 | sec 1095 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- Uploads or links a generated image with a question about quality

### INV-009772 | sec 1095 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- Asks "does this match the storyboard"

### INV-009773 | sec 1095 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- Says "review this render", "is this on-brand", "what should I change"

### INV-009774 | sec 1095 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- Has a generated image and a `shots.json` shot reference and wants QA

### INV-009775 | sec 1095 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- Has a generated image and just wants editorial feedback (no storyboard reference)

### INV-009776 | sec 1096 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
## What you produce

### INV-009777 | sec 1096 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
**Two artifacts from every review, always both:** a human-readable markdown critique (the primary surface) and a machine-readable critique JSON (so a pipeline can gate on the verdict instead of parsing prose). The JSON is detailed in Step 6; it never replaces the markdown.

### INV-009778 | sec 1096 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
The JSON goes to `output/critiques/round-{N}/{shot_id}.critique.json`. One file per shot
per round, never a shared filename. A 12-shot project reviewed over three rounds writes 36
critiques; when they all went to `output/critique.json` it kept one, and which one depended
on review order.

### INV-009779 | sec 1096 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
The markdown critique uses these sections:

### INV-009780 | sec 1096 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
```
## Verdict
ACCEPT / REVISE / REJECT, one line

## What's working
2–4 specific positives. Concrete observations, not flattery.

## What's not working
2–5 specific issues. Each one cites a layer. Brand Lock, Series Lock, Shot Spec, Composition, Technical, or Continuity.

## Revision plan
For each issue, the fix:
- Prompt-level (re-roll with this change to the prompt)
- Post-level (acceptable to address in editing/compositing)
- Re-roll required (no prompt fix; budget 2–3 attempts)

## Confidence
HIGH / MEDIUM / LOW, how sure you are about the verdict
```

### INV-009781 | sec 1097 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
## Inputs

### INV-009782 | sec 1097 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
You need:

### INV-009783 | sec 1097 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| Input | Required? | Default if absent |

### INV-009784 | sec 1097 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
|---|---|---|

### INV-009785 | sec 1097 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| The generated image | Yes | Cannot critique without it |

### INV-009786 | sec 1097 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| Shot ID + shots.json | Recommended | If absent, ask for shot intent in a sentence |

### INV-009787 | sec 1097 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| brand-lock.snapshot.md | Recommended | If absent, critique only on technical merits |

### INV-009788 | sec 1097 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| The original prompt used | Helpful | If absent, infer from intent |

### INV-009789 | sec 1097 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
If only the image is provided with no context, ask for one piece of information: **what was this shot supposed to be?** A single sentence is enough to anchor the critique.

### INV-009790 | sec 1098 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
## Workflow

### INV-009791 | sec 1099 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
### Step 1. Establish intent

### INV-009792 | sec 1099 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
What was this shot supposed to do? Pull from:

### INV-009793 | sec 1099 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- Shot's `rationale` field (if shots.json provided)

### INV-009794 | sec 1099 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- Shot's `subject`, `framing`, `angle`, `motion` fields

### INV-009795 | sec 1099 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- User's stated intent (if no shots.json)

### INV-009796 | sec 1099 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- The beat this shot serves

### INV-009797 | sec 1099 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
If you can't establish intent in one sentence, ask. Don't critique blind.

### INV-009798 | sec 1100 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
### Step 2. Critique by layer

### INV-009799 | sec 1100 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
Read `references/critique-rubric.md` for the full rubric. Quick version, check the image against:

### INV-009800 | sec 1100 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
1. **Brand Lock**, does it respect palette, mood, "never" list?

### INV-009801 | sec 1100 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
2. **Series Lock**, does it match character/environment/lighting anchors?

### INV-009802 | sec 1100 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
3. **Shot Spec**, does framing/angle/composition match the spec?

### INV-009803 | sec 1100 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
4. **Composition**, does it reserve space for on-screen text if applicable?

### INV-009804 | sec 1100 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
5. **Technical**, skin texture, hands, eyes, anatomy, AI artifacts?

### INV-009805 | sec 1100 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
6. **Continuity**, if previous shots in the series are available, does it match?

### INV-009806 | sec 1100 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
For each layer, note: pass / soft fail / hard fail. The verdict aggregates these.

### INV-009807 | sec 1101 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
### Step 3. Map issues to fixes

### INV-009808 | sec 1101 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
For every "not working" point, the critique must say what to do about it. Three buckets:

### INV-009809 | sec 1101 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
**Prompt-level fix**, change the prompt and re-roll. Specify the exact change:

### INV-009810 | sec 1101 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
> "The character has brown hair instead of salt-and-pepper. Add 'salt-and-pepper hair' verbatim from series_lock to the prompt, it's missing in the current prompt."

### INV-009811 | sec 1101 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
**Post-level fix**, acceptable to address in compositing. Specify what:

### INV-009812 | sec 1101 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
> "Color grade is slightly cool, push warmth +5 in post, no need to re-generate."

### INV-009813 | sec 1101 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
**Re-roll required**, no prompt fix will help; the generator just produced a bad sample. Budget 2–3 attempts:

### INV-009814 | sec 1101 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA | regla genérica de re-roll; Flux es el ejemplo
> "Hands are mangled. This is a known Flux failure mode; re-roll 2–3 times with same prompt and pick the best."

### INV-009815 | sec 1102 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
### Step 4. Verdict

### INV-009816 | sec 1102 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| Verdict | When |

### INV-009817 | sec 1102 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
|---|---|

### INV-009818 | sec 1102 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| ACCEPT | All layers pass or soft-fail in ways post can fix |

### INV-009819 | sec 1102 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| REVISE | One or two layers hard-fail; clear fix path |

### INV-009820 | sec 1102 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| REJECT | Three+ layers fail or one critical layer (Brand Lock, Series Lock) hard-fails with no clear fix |

### INV-009821 | sec 1103 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
### Step 5. Confidence

### INV-009822 | sec 1103 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
Be honest about uncertainty:

### INV-009823 | sec 1103 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| Confidence | When |

### INV-009824 | sec 1103 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
|---|---|

### INV-009825 | sec 1103 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| HIGH | Storyboard reference + brand-lock + prompt all available, clear assessment |

### INV-009826 | sec 1103 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| MEDIUM | Some references missing but core intent is clear |

### INV-009827 | sec 1103 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| LOW | Only the image, intent is inferred; verdict is your best guess |

### INV-009828 | sec 1103 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
HIGH is a factual claim about what you had, not a mood. `tools/validate_critique.py`
rejects a `1.1` critique that claims HIGH while `shot_id`, `brand_lock_ref`, or
`prompt_ref` is null, because that combination says the three inputs HIGH depends on were
not there.

### INV-009829 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
### Step 6. Emit structured output (critique JSON)

### INV-009830 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
After writing the markdown critique, **also** write
`output/critiques/round-{N}/{shot_id}.critique.json` conforming to
`templates/critique.schema.json` at version `1.1`. Same review, two surfaces. The markdown
is for the human; the JSON is so an automated QA loop (e.g. `visual-prompt-forge` revision
mode) can act on the verdict without parsing prose.

### INV-009831 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
**Map the markdown to the schema, section for section:**

### INV-009832 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| Markdown | JSON field |

### INV-009833 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
|---|---|

### INV-009834 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| `## Verdict` | `verdict` (`ACCEPT` / `REVISE` / `REJECT`) |

### INV-009835 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| `## What's working` bullets | `working[]` (one string each) |

### INV-009836 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| `## What's not working` + `## Revision plan` | `issues[]`, merge them: each issue carries its layer/note from "what's not working" and its `fix_type`/`fix` from the matching revision-plan line |

### INV-009837 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| `## Confidence` | `confidence` (`HIGH` / `MEDIUM` / `LOW`) |

### INV-009838 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
**Provenance, all of it required at 1.1.** A verdict is a claim about specific bytes. Name
them, and hash them:

### INV-009839 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| Field | Value |

### INV-009840 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
|---|---|

### INV-009841 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| `run_id`, `round` | from `run.json` and the round directory you are writing into |

### INV-009842 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| `created_at` | UTC instant, `YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ` |

### INV-009843 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| `shot_id` | the shot, or `null` for a standalone image with no storyboard |

### INV-009844 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| `image_ref`, `image_sha256` | the frame you reviewed, and its SHA-256 |

### INV-009845 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| `prompt_ref`, `prompt_sha256` | the prompt file it came from, and its SHA-256 |

### INV-009846 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| `brand_lock_ref`, `brand_lock_sha256` | the snapshot you judged against, and its SHA-256 |

### INV-009847 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| `generator`, `model_version` | the generator id from `_capabilities.json`, and its model version |

### INV-009848 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| `seed` | if the generator exposes one, else `null` |

### INV-009849 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
```bash
shasum -a 256 output/frames/round-1/shot_03.png \
              output/prompts/round-1/flux.txt \
              output/brand-lock.snapshot.md
```

### INV-009850 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
Every one of those fields is required, and every one is nullable. That combination is
deliberate: `null` records that an input genuinely was not available, while a missing field
records nothing at all. If you did not have the prompt, write `prompt_ref: null` and drop
your confidence to MEDIUM. Do not omit the key.

### INV-009851 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
The hashes are the point. Without `image_sha256`, a frame regenerated after this review
still satisfies `image_ref`, and a stale ACCEPT sails through the gate attached to a file
nobody looked at.

### INV-009852 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
**Severity, assign one per issue.** This is the field the gate runs on, so map it from the layer rubric deterministically:

### INV-009853 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| Severity | Means | Maps from |

### INV-009854 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
|---|---|---|

### INV-009855 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| `minor` | soft-fail, fixable in post | a soft-fail on any layer; `fix_type: post-level` |

### INV-009856 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| `major` | hard-fail **with** a clear fix path | a hard-fail that a prompt change or re-roll fixes |

### INV-009857 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
| `blocking` | hard-fail on a critical layer (Brand Lock / Series Lock) with **no** clear fix, or a defect that makes the asset unusable | an unrecoverable hard-fail |

### INV-009858 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
**Gating rule, the verdict is derived from severities, not chosen freely.** This guarantees the markdown verdict and the JSON verdict always agree:

### INV-009859 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- Any `blocking` issue ⇒ verdict is `REJECT`.

### INV-009860 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- Three or more `major` issues ⇒ verdict is `REJECT`.

### INV-009861 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- One or two `major` issues (and no blocking) ⇒ verdict is `REVISE`.

### INV-009862 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- Only `minor` issues, or none ⇒ verdict is `ACCEPT` (with post notes).

### INV-009863 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
The three-major rule used to read "escalate to REJECT at your discretion." Discretion in a
gate is not a gate, and it disagreed with `references/critique-rubric.md`, which called
three hard fails a REJECT outright. It is now a threshold, and the validator enforces it.

### INV-009864 | sec 1104 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
Pick the markdown `## Verdict` by this same rule.

### INV-009865 | sec 1105 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
### Step 7. Run the gate

### INV-009866 | sec 1105 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
Writing a schema-valid critique is not the same as passing the gate. Run it:

### INV-009867 | sec 1105 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
```bash
python tools/validate_critique.py output/critiques/round-1/shot_03.critique.json
```

### INV-009868 | sec 1105 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
Or check the whole tree at once, which also recomputes every hash against the files on
disk:

### INV-009869 | sec 1105 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
```bash
python tools/validate_provenance.py output/
```

### INV-009870 | sec 1105 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
This step is not optional and it is not someone else's job. A critique that says `ACCEPT`
while carrying a `major` issue is a bug, and the only reason to write a validator for that
bug is to actually run it. Before this step existed, the gate ran in CI against two
fixtures that ship in the repo and never once against a real client's critique.

### INV-009871 | sec 1105 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
Worked examples: `examples/critique.accept.json` and `examples/critique.revise.json` show
the shape at version `1.0`, which is still valid and carries no provenance.
`examples/worked-run/critiques/` shows version `1.1` with real hashes, two shots across two
rounds.

### INV-009872 | sec 1106 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
## Hard rules

### INV-009873 | sec 1107 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
### Rule 1. No vibes-based critique

### INV-009874 | sec 1107 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
"Looks great" / "feels off" without specifics is not a critique. Every observation must reference something in the image (composition, color, anatomy, lighting direction, etc.).

### INV-009875 | sec 1108 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
### Rule 2. Prompt-level fixes must be specific

### INV-009876 | sec 1108 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
"Change the prompt" is not a fix. "Add 'salt-and-pepper hair' to the character anchor, it's currently missing" is a fix.

### INV-009877 | sec 1109 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
### Rule 3. Don't critique what wasn't asked

### INV-009878 | sec 1109 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
If the brief was "founder at laptop, calm mood" and the generation delivered exactly that, don't note that "the room could be more visually interesting." That's scope creep, not critique.

### INV-009879 | sec 1110 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
### Rule 4. Be honest about generator limits

### INV-009880 | sec 1110 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
Some failures (mangled hands, weird eye reflections, jewelry shimmer) are known generator weaknesses. Surface them as such, don't pretend a different prompt will fix them. Recommend re-roll or post.

### INV-009881 | sec 1111 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
### Rule 5. When in doubt, ACCEPT and recommend post

### INV-009882 | sec 1111 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
Generation is one stage in a pipeline. If the image is 80% right and the gap is fixable in post, that's an ACCEPT with post notes. Don't send the user back to re-generate when an editor would handle it in 90 seconds.

### INV-009883 | sec 1112 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
## Reference

### INV-009884 | sec 1112 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- `references/critique-rubric.md`, the full layer-by-layer rubric

### INV-009885 | sec 1113 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
## Example output

### INV-009886 | sec 1113 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
> ## Verdict
> REVISE
>
> ## What's working
> - Composition correctly reserves the right two-thirds for text overlay (matches shot_03 rationale)
> - Lighting direction matches series_lock (window-left, warm afternoon)
> - Mood reads as calm/considered, on-brand
>
> ## What's not working
> - **Series Lock, character mismatch.** The character has brown hair, but series_lock specifies "salt-and-pepper." This is a verbatim-anchor failure.
> - **Technical, left hand.** Hand on the laptop has six fingers (Flux known failure mode).
> - **Shot Spec, framing.** Generated as MS but spec called for MCU. Subject is too small in frame.
>
> ## Revision plan
> - **Hair:** Prompt-level. Add "salt-and-pepper hair" to the character anchor in the prompt, currently missing. Re-roll.
> - **Hand:** Re-roll required. Generate 2–3 more times with same prompt and pick a clean one.
> - **Framing:** Prompt-level. Change "medium shot" to "medium close-up" in the prompt. Re-roll.
>
> ## Confidence
> HIGH

### INV-009887 | sec 1114 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
## Handoff

### INV-009888 | sec 1114 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
After delivering the critique, if the verdict is REVISE, offer:

### INV-009889 | sec 1114 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
> "Want me to draft the revised prompt? Point `visual-prompt-forge` at this output tree in
> revision mode and it will re-emit prompts for just the shots that need them."

### INV-009890 | sec 1114 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
If the verdict is REJECT, do not offer that. REJECT means a blocking issue or three or more
major ones, which is a failure with no clear fix path, and revision mode is built to stop
there and ask. Say what blocked it and what decision it needs: a changed shot spec, a
changed brand-lock, or a different generator.

### INV-009891 | sec 1114 | skills/visual-asset-critic/SKILL.md | ENTRA
Don't auto-revise. The user picks. The critique you just wrote is exactly what closes that
loop.

### INV-010313 | sec 1137 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
# Critique Rubric

### INV-010314 | sec 1137 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
The structured layer-by-layer pass. Use this as the checklist when reviewing a generated image.

### INV-010315 | sec 1138 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
## Layer 1. Brand Lock check

### INV-010316 | sec 1138 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Check | Pass | Soft fail | Hard fail |

### INV-010317 | sec 1138 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
|---|---|---|---|

### INV-010318 | sec 1138 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Palette | Image colors come from brand-lock palette | Colors close but slightly off | Colors not in palette at all |

### INV-010319 | sec 1138 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Mood adjectives | Image reads as the brand mood | Mood reads as adjacent (e.g. "calm" vs "neutral") | Image reads as a different mood (e.g. "energetic" when brief was "calm") |

### INV-010320 | sec 1138 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| "Never" list | None of the items in the never list are present | One item in the never list shows softly | Multiple never-list violations |

### INV-010321 | sec 1138 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Aspect ratio | Matches `project.aspect` | Within a crop of the spec | Wrong aspect |

### INV-010322 | sec 1138 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
Hard fail on Brand Lock = REJECT or REVISE depending on whether prompt fix exists.

### INV-010323 | sec 1139 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
## Layer 2. Series Lock check

### INV-010324 | sec 1139 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Check | Pass | Soft fail | Hard fail |

### INV-010325 | sec 1139 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
|---|---|---|---|

### INV-010326 | sec 1139 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Character anchor | All described features visible | One minor feature off (e.g. wrong shirt color) | Wrong person (different age/race/build than anchor) |

### INV-010327 | sec 1139 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Environment | Matches series_lock | Slight environment drift | Different environment entirely |

### INV-010328 | sec 1139 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Lighting direction | Matches series_lock | Lighting is right but slightly different angle | Lighting from wrong direction (continuity break) |

### INV-010329 | sec 1139 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Color grade | Matches series_lock | Slight tonal drift | Different color grade |

### INV-010330 | sec 1139 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
Hard fail on Series Lock = REJECT (continuity break) or REVISE if specific fix available.

### INV-010331 | sec 1140 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
## Layer 3. Shot Spec check

### INV-010332 | sec 1140 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Check | Pass | Soft fail | Hard fail |

### INV-010333 | sec 1140 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
|---|---|---|---|

### INV-010334 | sec 1140 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Framing | Matches spec (ECU/CU/MS/etc.) | One step off (e.g. MS instead of MCU) | Two+ steps off |

### INV-010335 | sec 1140 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Angle | Matches spec | Slight angle variation | Wrong angle (low when spec was eye-level) |

### INV-010336 | sec 1140 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Subject action | Matches `subject` description | Subject doing similar but slightly different action | Subject doing wrong action |

### INV-010337 | sec 1140 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Depth of field | Matches if specified | Slight DOF variation | Deep when shallow was specified |

### INV-010338 | sec 1140 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
Soft fail = post-level fix or accept. Hard fail = revise.

### INV-010339 | sec 1141 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
## Layer 4. Composition check

### INV-010340 | sec 1141 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Check | Pass | Soft fail | Hard fail |

### INV-010341 | sec 1141 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
|---|---|---|---|

### INV-010342 | sec 1141 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Negative space for text | Reserved per spec | Reserved but tight | No reserved space, overlay won't fit |

### INV-010343 | sec 1141 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Subject placement | Matches rationale | Slightly off (subject in left-third vs right-third) | Subject blocks where text was meant to go |

### INV-010344 | sec 1141 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Eye-line | Looks where intended | Slight gaze direction off | Looking the wrong way |

### INV-010345 | sec 1141 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Headroom | Appropriate | Slightly tight or loose | Cropped at hairline / huge headroom |

### INV-010346 | sec 1141 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
Composition fails are usually prompt-level fixable. Re-roll with explicit composition language.

### INV-010347 | sec 1142 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
## Layer 5. Technical check

### INV-010348 | sec 1142 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Check | Pass | Soft fail | Hard fail |

### INV-010349 | sec 1142 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
|---|---|---|---|

### INV-010350 | sec 1142 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Hands | Anatomically correct | Slight finger weirdness | Six fingers, fused fingers, wrong joints |

### INV-010351 | sec 1142 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Eyes | Symmetrical, focused | Slight asymmetry | Wonky eyes, wrong reflections |

### INV-010352 | sec 1142 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Skin texture | Natural | Slightly plastic | Heavy AI plastic |

### INV-010353 | sec 1142 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Anatomy | Correct | Minor weirdness | Major anatomy errors |

### INV-010354 | sec 1142 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Background artifacts | Clean | Minor weirdness | Distorted text, melted objects, impossible geometry |

### INV-010355 | sec 1142 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
Technical hard fails are almost always **re-roll required**. The prompt was probably fine; the generator just produced a bad sample. Budget 2–3 re-rolls.

### INV-010356 | sec 1143 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
## Layer 6. Continuity check (if previous shots available)

### INV-010357 | sec 1143 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Check | Pass | Soft fail | Hard fail |

### INV-010358 | sec 1143 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
|---|---|---|---|

### INV-010359 | sec 1143 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Character match | Same person, same look | Slight drift | Clearly different person |

### INV-010360 | sec 1143 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Lighting continuity | Same direction, time of day | Subtle shift | Direction reversed, time of day jumped |

### INV-010361 | sec 1143 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Environment continuity | Same space | Slight environment drift | Different space |

### INV-010362 | sec 1143 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Color grade match | Identical | Subtle shift | Visibly different |

### INV-010363 | sec 1143 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
Continuity hard fails break the storyboard. REVISE with verbatim-anchor checks on the prompt.

### INV-010364 | sec 1144 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
## From pass/fail to severity

### INV-010365 | sec 1144 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
The tables above grade each check as pass, soft fail, or hard fail. The JSON critique
records a severity instead, and the verdict is derived from those severities. Map them
this way, and only this way:

### INV-010366 | sec 1144 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Rubric result | Severity | Why |

### INV-010367 | sec 1144 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
|---|---|---|

### INV-010368 | sec 1144 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Soft fail on any layer | `minor` | Post can absorb it. `fix_type` is usually `post-level` |

### INV-010369 | sec 1144 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Hard fail **with** a clear fix path | `major` | A prompt change or a re-roll resolves it |

### INV-010370 | sec 1144 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Hard fail on Brand Lock or Series Lock **with no** fix path | `blocking` | Nothing downstream recovers this |

### INV-010371 | sec 1144 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Any defect that makes the asset unusable | `blocking` | Same, regardless of layer |

### INV-010372 | sec 1145 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
## Aggregating verdict

### INV-010373 | sec 1145 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
The verdict follows from the severities. It is not a separate judgement:

### INV-010374 | sec 1145 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| Severities present | Verdict |

### INV-010375 | sec 1145 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
|---|---|

### INV-010376 | sec 1145 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| any `blocking` | REJECT |

### INV-010377 | sec 1145 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| three or more `major` | REJECT |

### INV-010378 | sec 1145 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| one or two `major` | REVISE |

### INV-010379 | sec 1145 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
| only `minor`, or none | ACCEPT (with post notes) |

### INV-010380 | sec 1145 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
`tools/validate_critique.py` enforces exactly this table, so a critique that disagrees with
it fails rather than shipping. Earlier versions of this file counted hard fails and called
3+ a REJECT while the skill said "escalate at your discretion." Those two rules disagreed,
and the disagreement is the reason the threshold is now a number.

### INV-010381 | sec 1145 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
Soft fails are noted but don't change the verdict on their own. If you have three or more of
them, look again: a cluster of soft fails usually means one of them is really a hard fail
you talked yourself out of.

### INV-010382 | sec 1146 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
## Speed bumps to remember

### INV-010383 | sec 1146 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
- **Don't critique what wasn't asked**, if the spec didn't call for cinematic mood, don't say "could be more cinematic"

### INV-010384 | sec 1146 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
- **Don't pile-on once verdict is set**, if you're rejecting, list the issues that drive the rejection; don't list every cosmetic concern

### INV-010385 | sec 1146 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
- **Be specific, always**, "lighting is off" is not a critique; "key light is camera-right but series_lock says camera-left" is

### INV-010386 | sec 1146 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
- **Cite the layer**, every issue gets tagged with which layer it falls under. This makes the fix path obvious

### INV-010387 | sec 1146 | skills/visual-asset-critic/references/critique-rubric.md | ENTRA
- **Distinguish prompt failure from generator failure**, if the prompt was fine and the generator produced garbage hands, that's "re-roll required", not "fix the prompt"

### INV-011265 | sec 1205 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/SKILL.md | ENTRA
---
name: visual-prompt-forge
description: Generate model-specific prompts from shots.json. Outputs copy-paste-ready prompts for stills (Midjourney, Flux, Ideogram, GPT Image, Nano Banana, Seedream) and motion video (Kling, Veo, Seedance, Hailuo). Also runs a revision mode that reads a critique.json and re-emits prompts for only the failed shots, closing the QA loop. Use when the user asks for image or video prompts, mentions any of those generators, wants AI-generated frames for a storyboard, or hands over shots.json. The prompt half of the pipeline. Composes with storyboard-architect upstream, visual-asset-critic downstream.
---

### INV-011267 | sec 1206 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/SKILL.md | ENTRA
You are turning structured shot data into prompts that work in production. Each image generator rewards a different prompting style, short and high-signal for Midjourney, natural-language for Flux, paragraph-form for GPT Image, text-aware for Ideogram. A prompt that crushes in one will produce slop in another.

### INV-011272 | sec 1207 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- Names a specific generator (Midjourney, Flux, Ideogram, GPT Image, Nano Banana, Seedream, Kling, Veo, Seedance, Hailuo)

### INV-011273 | sec 1207 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/SKILL.md | ENTRA
- Asks for "image prompts," "Midjourney prompts," "AI prompts," "generation prompts" for a storyboard

### INV-011278 | sec 1208 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/SKILL.md | ENTRA

```
output/prompts/round-1/
├── midjourney.txt          # If targeted
├── flux.txt
├── ideogram.txt
├── gpt-image.txt
├── nano-banana.txt
├── seedream.txt
├── kling.txt               # Motion-aware video, default
├── veo.txt                 # Motion, dialogue/lipsync + native audio
├── seedance.txt            # Motion, multi-shot sequences
└── hailuo.txt              # Motion, budget iteration
```

### INV-011314 | sec 1212 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/SKILL.md | ENTRA | regla de validación de la herramienta; nano-banana es el ejemplo
That rule is now enforced rather than trusted. `tools/validate_capabilities.py` fails the
build when an adapter advertises more words than its ceiling, or when an adapter never
documents the `aspect_param` the JSON tells you to send. The second check exists because
nano-banana's matrix entry said `aspect_ratio` while its adapter said the API expects
`aspectRatio`; the precedence rule meant the wrong one won, silently, on every prompt.

### INV-011325 | sec 1214 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/SKILL.md | ENTRA | plantilla de salida para cualquier generador; midjourney es el ejemplo
```
# Storyboard: {project title}
# Generator: midjourney
# Model: {model_version from _capabilities.json}
# Aspect: 9:16
# Brand-lock: brand-lock.snapshot.md
# Run: {run_id from run.json}
# Round: 1

# shot_01, hook, 0.0-2.0s, MCU eye-level static
{the prompt}

# shot_02, pain, 2.0-6.0s, MS eye-level push
{the prompt}

...
```

### INV-011336 | sec 1216 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/SKILL.md | ENTRA | uso de copy-prompt.py; midjourney.txt es el ejemplo
```bash
python tools/copy-prompt.py output/prompts/round-1/midjourney.txt
python tools/copy-prompt.py output/prompts/round-2/revised-midjourney.txt --shot shot_03
```

### INV-011363 | sec 1223 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/SKILL.md | ENTRA | regla genérica de texto compuesto aparte; Ideogram es la excepción
If the shot has `on_screen_text: "text_03"`, the prompt does NOT contain the text content. Text is composited separately. The only exception: Ideogram, where text-in-image is the reason you'd choose it, but even then, treat it as an explicit override flagged in rationale.

### INV-011375 | sec 1226 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/SKILL.md | ENTRA | el adaptador manda sobre el entrenamiento; Midjourney es el ejemplo
If your training data says Midjourney uses `--style 4a` and the adapter file says `--style raw`, the adapter wins. Image-gen syntax changes monthly. The adapter is current; your training is not.

### INV-011406 | sec 1231 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/SKILL.md | ENTRA | uso de validate_prompts.py y copy-prompt.py; flux.txt es el ejemplo
```bash
python tools/validate_prompts.py output/
python tools/copy-prompt.py output/prompts/round-1/flux.txt --list
```

### INV-011424 | sec 1232 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/SKILL.md | ENTRA
`examples/one-shot-all-adapters/` contains a single shot rendered across seven adapters
side-by-side: the six stills generators plus Kling. Use it to calibrate output quality.
The three remaining motion adapters (Veo, Seedance, Hailuo) have no worked example yet;
their `.md` files carry a worked prompt each in the meantime.

### INV-011718 | sec 1246 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/adapters/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
# Adapter: GPT Image (1.5 / 2)

### INV-011720 | sec 1246 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/adapters/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
GPT Image rewards paragraph-form prompts with explicit spatial reasoning and complex composition descriptions. It interprets relational language ("to the left of", "behind", "in the foreground") more accurately than any other generator. Choose GPT Image when the brief requires precise scene composition, multiple objects, or accurate text rendering.

### INV-011723 | sec 1247 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/adapters/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
Paragraph breaks help GPT Image parse separate beats. One long monolithic paragraph underperforms, the model loses track of which detail applies where.

### INV-011725 | sec 1248 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/adapters/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
GPT Image is API-driven through OpenAI:

### INV-011731 | sec 1248 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/adapters/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
| `model` | `gpt-image-1.5` (or `gpt-image-2` if available) | |

### INV-011733 | sec 1248 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/adapters/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
```
# shot_01, params: size=1024x1792, quality=high, style=natural, model=gpt-image-1.5
{prompt}
```

### INV-011735 | sec 1249 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/adapters/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
GPT Image handles **150–300 words** comfortably. Longer than other generators. Use the headroom for explicit spatial descriptions.

### INV-011737 | sec 1250 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/adapters/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
GPT Image's distinctive capability, describe spatial relationships explicitly:

### INV-011749 | sec 1253 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/adapters/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
GPT Image handles text-in-image at roughly 95% accuracy, second only to Ideogram. When text is required:

### INV-011754 | sec 1253 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/adapters/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
Default for storyboard work is still composited text, keep the override flag pattern from the Ideogram adapter.

### INV-011756 | sec 1254 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/adapters/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
- **Don't write Midjourney-style comma stacks**. GPT Image parses them as a list of disconnected concepts

### INV-011759 | sec 1254 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/adapters/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
- **Don't include `--ar` flags or weight syntax**. GPT Image ignores them

### INV-011760 | sec 1254 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/adapters/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
- **Don't forget that GPT Image's "AI look" is real**, the closing "natural skin texture, no AI rendering artifacts" line meaningfully helps but isn't a complete fix. For pure photoreal, Flux is still stronger

### INV-011762 | sec 1255 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/adapters/gpt-image.md | ENTRA
GPT Image is OpenAI-only as of Q2 2026. Standard `images.generate` endpoint. Pricing roughly $0.04–0.08 per image depending on size and quality. ChatGPT Plus and above gives UI access.

### INV-012202 | sec 1344 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/gpt-image.txt | SALE | ejemplo generado para gpt-image-1.5 (cabecera del documento)
# Storyboard: WhyStrohm, The Content Infrastructure Pitch
# Generator: gpt-image (1.5)
# Aspect: 9:16
# Brand-lock: brand-lock.snapshot.md

### INV-012217 | sec 1350 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
# Consistency Locks

### INV-012218 | sec 1350 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
Visual consistency across a storyboard is the hardest problem in AI image generation. Generators are stateless, every prompt is interpreted fresh. Without consistency locks, shot 1 and shot 7 will look like different productions.

### INV-012219 | sec 1350 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
This document covers the techniques that actually work in 2026.

### INV-012220 | sec 1351 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
## Lock 1. Verbatim character anchors

### INV-012221 | sec 1351 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
The most effective single technique. The character is described in `series_lock.character` once, and that exact string appears in every shot's prompt verbatim. No paraphrasing, no synonyms, no "improvement."

### INV-012222 | sec 1351 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
**Good (consistent):**

### INV-012223 | sec 1351 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
```
shot_01: "founder, mid-thirties, salt-and-pepper hair, navy crewneck, stubble"
shot_02: "founder, mid-thirties, salt-and-pepper hair, navy crewneck, stubble"
shot_07: "founder, mid-thirties, salt-and-pepper hair, navy crewneck, stubble"
```

### INV-012224 | sec 1351 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
**Bad (inconsistent):**

### INV-012225 | sec 1351 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
```
shot_01: "a thoughtful entrepreneur in his thirties, dark hair, casual sweater"
shot_02: "founder, 35 years old, salt-pepper hair, blue knit top"
shot_07: "the man in the home office, navy shirt"
```

### INV-012226 | sec 1351 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
Even small variations compound. Lock the string. Repeat verbatim.

### INV-012227 | sec 1352 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
## Lock 2. Verbatim environment anchors

### INV-012228 | sec 1352 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
Same principle for `series_lock.environment`. The environment string is identical in every shot prompt. The shot's individual subject describes what changes within the environment, not the environment itself.

### INV-012229 | sec 1353 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
## Lock 3. Verbatim lighting anchors

### INV-012230 | sec 1353 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
Same principle for `series_lock.lighting`. Lighting direction in particular is critical, flipping window-left to window-right between shots produces obvious cuts where there shouldn't be any.

### INV-012231 | sec 1354 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | SALE | título «Lock 4. Seed locking»: seed no existe en GPT Image
## Lock 4. Seed locking

### INV-012232 | sec 1354 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | SALE | seed lock: Flux, Seedream, Ideogram, Stable Diffusion
For generators that support seeds (Flux, Seedream, Ideogram, Stable Diffusion), set the seed at the storyboard level. Same seed across every shot. This anchors the generator's randomness so character features carry.

### INV-012233 | sec 1354 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | SALE | seed en shots.json: no existe en GPT Image
In `shots.json`, you can document the seed at the project level:

### INV-012234 | sec 1354 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | SALE | ejemplo JSON de seed: no existe en GPT Image
```json
{
  "project": {
    "title": "...",
    "seed": 2840193
  }
}
```

### INV-012235 | sec 1354 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | SALE | el adaptador aplica el seed: no existe en GPT Image
The adapter reads this and applies to every prompt.

### INV-012236 | sec 1354 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | SALE | seeds en Midjourney, Veo y Seedance
**Caveat:** Midjourney and some motion models (Veo, Seedance) handle seeds differently, or not at all. Document seed-locking as best-effort, not guaranteed.

### INV-012237 | sec 1355 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
## Lock 5. Reference images (`--cref`, omni-reference, image-to-image)

### INV-012238 | sec 1355 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | SALE | referencia de imagen en Midjourney v7, Ideogram v3 y Nano Banana
For Midjourney v7, Ideogram v3, and Nano Banana, you can pass a reference image alongside the prompt to anchor character or style.

### INV-012239 | sec 1355 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
**Workflow:**

### INV-012240 | sec 1355 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
1. Generate the hero shot first (any shot, usually `shot_01` or whichever is most defining)

### INV-012241 | sec 1355 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
2. Use that image as the reference for every subsequent shot

### INV-012242 | sec 1355 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
3. Each subsequent prompt has the verbatim character anchor PLUS the reference image link

### INV-012243 | sec 1355 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | SALE | Midjourney --cref
For Midjourney: `--cref {url} --cw 50` (character weight 50 = features only, not clothing).

### INV-012244 | sec 1355 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | SALE | Ideogram omni-reference
For Ideogram: `omni-reference` parameter with the image.

### INV-012245 | sec 1355 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | SALE | Nano Banana referenceImages
For Nano Banana: `referenceImages` array.

### INV-012246 | sec 1355 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
**This is the most effective lock for character consistency** in 2026. Use it whenever the storyboard features the same person across multiple shots.

### INV-012247 | sec 1356 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
## Lock 6. Style references

### INV-012248 | sec 1356 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
When the storyboard has a distinctive visual style (specific film stock, specific lighting school, specific color theory), use a style reference image:

### INV-012249 | sec 1356 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | SALE | Midjourney --sref
- Midjourney: `--sref {url}`

### INV-012250 | sec 1356 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | SALE | Ideogram style reference
- Ideogram: style reference parameter

### INV-012251 | sec 1356 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | SALE | Flux sin style reference
- Flux: not directly supported; bake style language into prompt instead

### INV-012252 | sec 1356 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
A consistent style reference across all shots produces aesthetic continuity even when characters or environments shift.

### INV-012253 | sec 1357 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
## Lock 7. Lighting direction continuity

### INV-012254 | sec 1357 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
A subtle one that breaks scenes when violated. If `series_lock.lighting` says "soft natural side-light, large window LEFT", then every shot's lighting must respect that direction.

### INV-012255 | sec 1357 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
When a shot reverses character orientation (e.g., over-the-shoulder reverse), update the rationale to acknowledge the lighting flip:

### INV-012256 | sec 1357 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
```
"rationale": "OTS reverse, light source now camera-right (matching scene continuity from window-left in master)"
```

### INV-012257 | sec 1357 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
If you find yourself flipping light direction without rationale, you're producing visual jump cuts.

### INV-012258 | sec 1358 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
## Lock 8. Color grade flow-through

### INV-012259 | sec 1358 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
The `series_lock.color_grade` string flows into every prompt verbatim. Same as character/environment/lighting. If shot 1 is "warm filmic, muted teal shadows" and shot 7 is "cinematic teal and orange", the audience will read it as different scenes.

### INV-012260 | sec 1359 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
## Failure modes when locks are missing

### INV-012261 | sec 1359 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
| Missing lock | Failure mode |

### INV-012262 | sec 1359 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
|---|---|

### INV-012263 | sec 1359 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
| Character | Different person every shot |

### INV-012264 | sec 1359 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
| Environment | Scene jumps |

### INV-012265 | sec 1359 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
| Lighting | Time-of-day jumps, eye-line breaks |

### INV-012266 | sec 1359 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | SALE | fila «Seed | Random feature drift»: no existe en GPT Image
| Seed | Random feature drift |

### INV-012267 | sec 1359 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
| Reference image | Loose character interpretation |

### INV-012268 | sec 1359 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
| Color grade | Tonal whiplash |

### INV-012269 | sec 1359 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
Every one of these is a production problem editorial cannot fully fix. Lock at the prompt level. Save the editor's time.

### INV-012270 | sec 1360 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
## What doesn't work

### INV-012271 | sec 1360 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
A few things people try that don't actually fix consistency:

### INV-012272 | sec 1360 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA | «same person no sirve» choca con multi-panel.md:308, que lo exige dentro de una sola imagen
- **Adding "consistent character" or "same person" to the prompt**, generators don't read meta-instructions

### INV-012273 | sec 1360 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
- **Numbering shots in the prompt** ("shot 3 of 7, same as before"), generators don't have memory across calls

### INV-012274 | sec 1360 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
- **Long descriptive paragraphs of the character**, past 4–5 features, the generator starts dropping details randomly

### INV-012275 | sec 1360 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
- **Asking for "exactly the same"**, there is no such thing in stateless image gen; you reduce drift, you don't eliminate it

### INV-012276 | sec 1360 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/consistency-locks.md | ENTRA
Accept that some shots will need re-rolls. Budget for it. The locks reduce the failure rate; they don't eliminate it.

### INV-012277 | sec 1361 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
# Failure Modes

### INV-012278 | sec 1361 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
What goes wrong when generated images don't match the storyboard, and what to fix at the prompt level vs. accept as editorial work.

### INV-012279 | sec 1362 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
## Failure 1. The "AI look"

### INV-012280 | sec 1362 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Symptoms:** plastic skin, oversaturated colors, eyes too symmetrical, hands wrong, generic stock-photo composition, lighting that's too even, "rendered" feel.

### INV-012281 | sec 1362 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Causes:**

### INV-012282 | sec 1362 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Generic mood adjectives ("beautiful", "stunning", "amazing")

### INV-012283 | sec 1362 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA | las muletillas de Stable Diffusion se prohíben en los demás generadores, GPT Image incluido
- Stable Diffusion crutches ("4k", "highly detailed", "masterpiece") in non-SD generators

### INV-012284 | sec 1362 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Missing photographic specifics

### INV-012285 | sec 1362 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- No "never" list applied from brand-lock

### INV-012286 | sec 1362 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Prompt-level fixes:**

### INV-012287 | sec 1362 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA | «50mm prime, f/2.0»: números de lente; Nano Banana los ignora, GPT Image 2 solo como look (prompt-framework.md:42)
- Add specific lens and aperture ("50mm prime, f/2.0")

### INV-012288 | sec 1362 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Add film stock or camera reference ("Sony FX6", "Kodak Portra 400 film stock")

### INV-012289 | sec 1362 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Add "natural skin texture, no AI rendering artifacts" closing line (Flux, GPT Image)

### INV-012290 | sec 1362 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Verify brand-lock "never" list is being applied, if it includes "no AI uncanny", surface that into the prompt

### INV-012291 | sec 1362 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | SALE | hero shots: Flux 2 Pro o Midjourney v7
- Use Flux 2 Pro or Midjourney v7 instead of cheaper models for hero shots

### INV-012292 | sec 1362 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Accept editorially when:** the brief is for stylized content where realism isn't the goal.

### INV-012293 | sec 1363 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
## Failure 2. Character drift across shots

### INV-012294 | sec 1363 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Symptoms:** same person looks different in shot 1 vs shot 5; clothing changes; hair color shifts; age perceived differently.

### INV-012295 | sec 1363 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Causes:**

### INV-012296 | sec 1363 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Series_lock character string paraphrased rather than verbatim

### INV-012297 | sec 1363 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- No reference image passed (`--cref`, omni-reference)

### INV-012298 | sec 1363 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | SALE | causa «Seed not locked»: no existe en GPT Image
- Seed not locked

### INV-012299 | sec 1363 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Too few features described in character anchor (gives generator too much room)

### INV-012300 | sec 1363 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Prompt-level fixes:**

### INV-012301 | sec 1363 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Verify character string is verbatim across every prompt

### INV-012302 | sec 1363 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Add 1–2 more specific features ("small scar above left eyebrow", "black wireframe glasses")

### INV-012303 | sec 1363 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Use reference image after the first successful shot

### INV-012304 | sec 1363 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | SALE | arreglo «Lock seed at storyboard level»: no existe en GPT Image
- Lock seed at storyboard level

### INV-012305 | sec 1363 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | SALE | Midjourney --cref --cw 50
- For Midjourney, use `--cref` with `--cw 50`

### INV-012306 | sec 1363 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Accept editorially when:** character consistency is genuinely impossible to lock. Budget re-rolls, typically 2–3× per problematic shot.

### INV-012307 | sec 1364 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
## Failure 3. Text in image looks bad

### INV-012308 | sec 1364 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Symptoms:** text is misspelled, kerning is off, font is wrong, multiple text elements compete.

### INV-012309 | sec 1364 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Causes:**

### INV-012310 | sec 1364 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Text was put in the image prompt instead of being composited

### INV-012311 | sec 1364 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | SALE | Midjourney para trabajo con texto
- Wrong generator chosen (using Midjourney for text-heavy work)

### INV-012312 | sec 1364 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Multiple text elements in one prompt

### INV-012313 | sec 1364 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Prompt-level fixes:**

### INV-012314 | sec 1364 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- The right answer is almost always: **don't put text in the prompt**. Composite separately from `text-overlays.json`.

### INV-012315 | sec 1364 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | SALE | texto en imagen: Ideogram v3
- If text must be in-image (poster work), use Ideogram v3 with explicit override flag

### INV-012316 | sec 1364 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Limit to one text element per prompt

### INV-012317 | sec 1364 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA | fuentes simples para texto en imagen, en cualquier generador
- Use simple fonts (sans-serif, clean serif), cursive and decorative fonts fail even on Ideogram

### INV-012318 | sec 1364 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | SALE | aceptar el resultado de Ideogram
**Accept editorially when:** the deliverable genuinely requires baked-in text and Ideogram's output is close enough to retouch.

### INV-012319 | sec 1365 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
## Failure 4. Lighting direction inconsistency

### INV-012320 | sec 1365 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Symptoms:** shot 3 has light from window-left, shot 4 has light from window-right, they cut together as a continuity error.

### INV-012321 | sec 1365 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Causes:**

### INV-012322 | sec 1365 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Lighting language paraphrased rather than verbatim from series_lock

### INV-012323 | sec 1365 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Reverse-angle shots not flagged in rationale

### INV-012324 | sec 1365 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Generator interpreting lighting ambiguously

### INV-012325 | sec 1365 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Prompt-level fixes:**

### INV-012326 | sec 1365 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Verify lighting string is verbatim across prompts

### INV-012327 | sec 1365 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Be explicit about direction: "from camera-left" not "from one side"

### INV-012328 | sec 1365 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- For reverse-angle shots, flag in rationale and update lighting language for that shot only

### INV-012329 | sec 1365 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- For Flux and GPT Image, add the photographic spec ("key light camera-left at 45 degrees")

### INV-012330 | sec 1365 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Accept editorially when:** lighting drift is subtle enough that color grading will fix it.

### INV-012331 | sec 1366 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
## Failure 5. Composition doesn't reserve space for text overlay

### INV-012332 | sec 1366 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Symptoms:** the storyboard says text goes in the right third, but the generated image has the subject filling the right third.

### INV-012333 | sec 1366 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Causes:**

### INV-012334 | sec 1366 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Shot subject didn't include negative-space note

### INV-012335 | sec 1366 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Generator interpreting "centered subject" by default

### INV-012336 | sec 1366 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Prompt-level fixes:**

### INV-012337 | sec 1366 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Add explicit composition note: "subject framed in left third, right two-thirds open for text overlay"

### INV-012338 | sec 1366 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- For GPT Image (best at spatial reasoning), use percentage references: "subject at left 30% of frame"

### INV-012339 | sec 1366 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Re-roll with explicit composition prompt, this often fixes on second try

### INV-012340 | sec 1366 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Accept editorially when:** image is otherwise great. Reposition text overlay to fit the actual composition.

### INV-012341 | sec 1367 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
## Failure 6. Series feels "different" even with locks applied

### INV-012342 | sec 1367 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Symptoms:** all locks are in place, prompts look right, but the storyboard feels disjointed when viewed end-to-end.

### INV-012343 | sec 1367 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Causes:**

### INV-012344 | sec 1367 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Color grade language drifting between shots (one says "warm filmic", another says "cinematic")

### INV-012345 | sec 1367 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Aspect ratio drift (some 9:16 generations are taller than others)

### INV-012346 | sec 1367 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Mood adjectives shifting ("calm" in shot 1 vs "energetic" in shot 5 even though brief was uniform)

### INV-012347 | sec 1367 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Prompt-level fixes:**

### INV-012348 | sec 1367 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Audit every prompt against series_lock and brand-lock, verbatim check

### INV-012349 | sec 1367 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Verify aspect ratio is identical across all prompts

### INV-012350 | sec 1367 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Lock the brand mood adjectives, same set in every prompt

### INV-012351 | sec 1367 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Accept editorially when:** the disjoint feel is below the noise floor for the medium (e.g. fast-cut social where each shot is on screen <2s).

### INV-012352 | sec 1368 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
## Failure 7, "Looks fine but doesn't match the brief"

### INV-012353 | sec 1368 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Symptoms:** image is technically good but doesn't capture what the brief was actually about.

### INV-012354 | sec 1368 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Causes:**

### INV-012355 | sec 1368 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- The shot subject in `shots.json` was vague

### INV-012356 | sec 1368 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- The brief context wasn't surfaced into the prompt

### INV-012357 | sec 1368 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- The generator is producing competent generic output rather than specific intent

### INV-012358 | sec 1368 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Prompt-level fixes:**

### INV-012359 | sec 1368 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Tighten the shot's subject field, what specifically is the audience meant to feel or notice

### INV-012360 | sec 1368 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- Include a one-phrase intent at the end ("the moment of recognition", "the calm before the decision")

### INV-012361 | sec 1368 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
- For complex scenes, add a "what this shot conveys" line

### INV-012362 | sec 1368 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
**Accept editorially when:** generation is close enough that retouching gets there. Don't burn API credits chasing perfect on-prompt.

### INV-012363 | sec 1369 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
## When to stop iterating prompts

### INV-012364 | sec 1369 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
Three signs:

### INV-012365 | sec 1369 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
1. You've re-rolled the same shot 5+ times with no improvement → the prompt is fine; the model can't render this concept; pick a different shot or different generator

### INV-012366 | sec 1369 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA | rendimientos decrecientes del prompt; NB es una de las dos salidas, la otra es postproducción
2. You're tweaking single words and hoping → you've hit prompt-level diminishing returns; move to image-to-image refinement (Nano Banana) or post-production

### INV-012367 | sec 1369 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
3. The image is 80% right and the gap is editorial → ship to post and let the editor finish

### INV-012368 | sec 1369 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/failure-modes.md | ENTRA
Generation is one stage in a pipeline. Don't try to make it the whole pipeline.

### INV-012369 | sec 1370 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
# Prompt Anatomy: The Five Layers

### INV-012370 | sec 1370 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
Every image prompt this skill produces is composed from five layers. Four are inputs, one is applied at compose-time. Understanding the layers is the difference between bulletproof prompts and lottery tickets.

### INV-012371 | sec 1371 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
## Layer 1. Brand Lock

### INV-012372 | sec 1371 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
**What:** the locked brand parameters for the entire project.
**Source:** `brand-lock.snapshot.md` referenced in `shots.json`.
**Constant across:** every storyboard, every shot.

### INV-012373 | sec 1371 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
Contains:

### INV-012374 | sec 1371 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Palette (hex values)

### INV-012375 | sec 1371 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Typography (font names, weights)

### INV-012376 | sec 1371 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Mood descriptors (3–5 adjectives that describe the brand's emotional posture)

### INV-012377 | sec 1371 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- "Never" list (things this brand never does, e.g. "no stock photo aesthetic", "no AI uncanny", "no over-saturated", "no shouting copy")

### INV-012378 | sec 1371 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Aspect ratios used

### INV-012379 | sec 1371 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Color grade direction

### INV-012380 | sec 1371 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
This layer answers: **what does the brand always look and feel like?**

### INV-012381 | sec 1372 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
## Layer 2. Series Lock

### INV-012382 | sec 1372 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
**What:** the locked anchors for this specific storyboard.
**Source:** `shots.json` → `series_lock`.
**Constant across:** every shot in this one storyboard.

### INV-012383 | sec 1372 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
Contains:

### INV-012384 | sec 1372 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Character anchor (who's in frame, described identically every shot)

### INV-012385 | sec 1372 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Environment description (where the action takes place, identical every shot)

### INV-012386 | sec 1372 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Lighting setup (direction, source, color temperature, identical every shot)

### INV-012387 | sec 1372 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Color grade (filmic look, identical every shot)

### INV-012388 | sec 1372 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
This layer answers: **what stays the same across this storyboard?**

### INV-012389 | sec 1372 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
The series_lock is what makes shot 1 and shot 7 feel like the same piece. Without it, every generated frame looks like a different production.

### INV-012390 | sec 1373 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
## Layer 3. Shot Spec

### INV-012391 | sec 1373 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
**What:** the per-shot variables.
**Source:** `shots.json` → `shots[i]`.
**Variable across:** every shot.

### INV-012392 | sec 1373 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
Contains:

### INV-012393 | sec 1373 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Framing (ECU, CU, MS, etc.)

### INV-012394 | sec 1373 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Angle (eye-level, high, low, etc.)

### INV-012395 | sec 1373 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Motion (static, push, pull, etc.)

### INV-012396 | sec 1373 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Subject (what's happening in this specific shot)

### INV-012397 | sec 1373 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Depth of field

### INV-012398 | sec 1373 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Rationale (audit trail, why this shot, this beat, this moment)

### INV-012399 | sec 1373 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
This layer answers: **what's different about this specific shot?**

### INV-012400 | sec 1374 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
## Layer 4. Text Layer

### INV-012401 | sec 1374 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA | el texto nunca va en el prompt; Ideogram es la excepción
**What:** on-screen text composited after generation.
**Source:** `text-overlays.json`.
**Never appears in image prompts** (except Ideogram Mode 2 with explicit override).

### INV-012402 | sec 1374 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
Why text is a separate layer:

### INV-012403 | sec 1374 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
1. **Editability.** Composited text can be revised without re-generating images.

### INV-012404 | sec 1374 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
2. **Quality.** Even Ideogram and GPT Image (the best at text) produce typography that lags professional design tools.

### INV-012405 | sec 1374 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
3. **Animation.** Animated text needs After Effects / Remotion / CapCut. Static rendered text is dead-on-arrival for motion content.

### INV-012406 | sec 1374 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
4. **Brand control.** Composited text uses exact brand fonts and exact brand colors. Generated text approximates.

### INV-012407 | sec 1375 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
## Layer 5. Generator Adapter

### INV-012408 | sec 1375 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
**What:** model-specific syntax wrapper.
**Source:** the relevant `adapters/{generator}.md` file.
**Variable across:** every target generator.

### INV-012409 | sec 1375 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
This is the only layer the skill *applies* (vs. reads). Layers 1–4 are inputs; Layer 5 is the renderer that turns inputs into a generator-specific string.

### INV-012410 | sec 1375 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
The adapter handles:

### INV-012411 | sec 1375 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | SALE | orden de palabras en Midjourney y Kling
- Word order (Midjourney leads with subject; motion adapters like Kling lead with camera motion)

### INV-012412 | sec 1375 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Length conventions (Seedream short, GPT Image long)

### INV-012413 | sec 1375 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
- Parameter syntax (`--ar 9:16` vs `aspect_ratio: "9:16"`)

### INV-012414 | sec 1375 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | SALE | fortalezas de Ideogram y Flux
- Idiosyncratic strengths (Ideogram for text, Flux for photoreal, etc.)

### INV-012415 | sec 1376 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
## How layers compose

### INV-012416 | sec 1376 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
For a single shot's prompt:

### INV-012417 | sec 1376 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
```
Layer 1 (brand) ───┐
                   │
Layer 2 (series) ──┼──> Layer 5 (adapter) ──> {generator-specific prompt}
                   │
Layer 3 (shot) ────┘

Layer 4 (text) ────> separate compositing pipeline
```

### INV-012418 | sec 1376 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
The adapter pulls from Layers 1–3 and produces a string. Layer 4 lives parallel and never enters the prompt.

### INV-012419 | sec 1377 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
## Why this matters operationally

### INV-012420 | sec 1377 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
**Change the brand color** → edit brand-lock.md. Every prompt across every storyboard updates. No find-and-replace across files.

### INV-012421 | sec 1377 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
**Change the character** → edit series_lock in shots.json. Every shot in this storyboard updates. Other storyboards untouched.

### INV-012422 | sec 1377 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
**Change one shot** → edit just that shot's spec. Other shots unaffected.

### INV-012423 | sec 1377 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
**Change the generator** → swap adapters. Same shot data, different output file. Zero edits to brand-lock or series_lock.

### INV-012424 | sec 1377 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
**Audit a render** → check brand-lock.snapshot.md to see what version the storyboard was built against.

### INV-012425 | sec 1377 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/references/prompt-anatomy.md | ENTRA
This is why guardrails-in-code beat guardrails-in-vibes. Each layer has one job. Changes are surgical. Outputs are reproducible.
