### INV-000523 | sec 16 | flujos/flujo-spot.html | MIXTA ENTRA
Cada flecha es una herencia con referencia adjunta en ese prompt. El cuadro T3 es el punto donde convergen las seis clases: un sujeto canonizado dentro de un mundo canonizado, con el producto y el objeto ancla ya fijados. Un error en cualquier génesis se propaga a todo lo que hay a la derecha.

### INV-000698 | sec 34 | skills/ai-production-director/SKILL.md | MIXTA ENTRA | vocabulario prohibido en todo prompt final
### 6.1 Vocabulario

### INV-000699 | sec 34 | skills/ai-production-director/SKILL.md | MIXTA ENTRA | vocabulario prohibido en todo prompt final
El vocabulario prohibido de `video`/dramaturgy (+ `aurora-prompt-linter` si está) **gana siempre**: "cinematic", "epic", "stunning", "masterpiece", "beautiful lighting", "professional", "high quality", emociones nombradas sin cuerpo. Esto **anula explícitamente** la regla de `ai-video-storyboard` de cerrar cada prompt con "cinematic 1080p, synchronized audio". En su lugar: la resolución, aspect ratio, duración y si el modelo genera audio se especifican como parámetros técnicos concretos según el archivo del modelo destino.

### INV-000700 | sec 35 | skills/ai-production-director/SKILL.md | MIXTA ENTRA | todo prompt final en la sintaxis del modelo destino
### 6.2 Sintaxis de modelo

### INV-000701 | sec 35 | skills/ai-production-director/SKILL.md | MIXTA ENTRA | todo prompt final en la sintaxis del modelo destino
`ai-video-storyboard` pide prompts model-agnostic; `video`/`image` (smixs) existen para sintaxis por modelo. Resolución: model-agnostic SOLO en artefactos intermedios (storyboard, shot cards). Todo prompt FINAL se escribe en la sintaxis del modelo destino según el archivo smixs correspondiente. Si el modelo no tiene archivo smixs (p.ej. Flux, Midjourney, Ideogram), la autoridad es el adaptador de `visual-prompt-forge`.

### INV-000704 | sec 37 | skills/ai-production-director/SKILL.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el borrador de forge se reescribe a la sintaxis del skill image
### 6.4 División forge ↔ smixs

### INV-000705 | sec 37 | skills/ai-production-director/SKILL.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el borrador de forge se reescribe a la sintaxis del skill image
`visual-prompt-forge` es dueño del **flujo de datos** (shots.json → borrador, critique.json → revisión selectiva). `video`/`image` smixs son dueños del **texto final** del prompt. El borrador del forge se reescribe a sintaxis smixs; nunca al revés.

### INV-002885 | sec 348 | skills/image/references/patterns/portrait-cinema.md | MIXTA ENTRA
## Japanese Negative Film Rooftop Portrait

### INV-002886 | sec 348 | skills/image/references/patterns/portrait-cinema.md | MIXTA ENTRA
Use for moody, atmospheric portraits with overexposed analog film qualities — muted colors, lifted shadows, and a feeling of faded memory. Ideal for editorial, zine, or personal project work.

### INV-002887 | sec 348 | skills/image/references/patterns/portrait-cinema.md | MIXTA ENTRA
<!-- Source concept: Japanese negative film aesthetic — overexposed, muted tones, rooftop setting -->

### INV-002888 | sec 348 | skills/image/references/patterns/portrait-cinema.md | MIXTA ENTRA
A waist-up portrait of {person_description} on a {rooftop_description}. They stand near the edge railing, {pose_description}, with the {city_skyline} visible behind them but washed out and desaturated by {sky_condition}. Shot on expired Japanese negative film — colors shifted toward {color_shift}, highlights blown soft and chalky, shadows lifted with visible grain in the flat midtones. Skin tones slightly green-yellow as if the film has aged. {clothing_description} reads as muted tones, almost monochromatic against the overexposed sky. Wind moves {wind_detail}. The mood is nostalgic and transient — a memory captured on deteriorating film stock. Eye-level framing, subject slightly off-center toward {frame_position}. Format: {aspect_ratio}.

### INV-002889 | sec 348 | skills/image/references/patterns/portrait-cinema.md | MIXTA ENTRA
**Key levers:** `{person_description}`, `{rooftop_description}` (concrete apartment building rooftop, industrial warehouse roof with exhaust vents, school building roof with chain-link fence), `{pose_description}` (leaning on railing looking at camera, turned away looking at skyline, sitting on a ledge with knees drawn up), `{city_skyline}` (Tokyo mid-rise apartments, generic Asian city with power lines), `{sky_condition}` (overcast white sky, hazy afternoon sun), `{color_shift}` (green-cyan cast, yellow-amber cast), `{clothing_description}` (oversized vintage windbreaker, plain white t-shirt, navy work jacket), `{wind_detail}` (hair across face, jacket hem, collar), `{frame_position}` (left third, right third), `{aspect_ratio}` (3:2, 4:5)

### INV-002890 | sec 348 | skills/image/references/patterns/portrait-cinema.md | MIXTA SALE | «Recommended model: NB2»
**Recommended model:** NB2 — analog film grain emulation and atmospheric mood

### INV-002891 | sec 348 | skills/image/references/patterns/portrait-cinema.md | MIXTA ENTRA
---

### INV-002892 | sec 349 | skills/image/references/patterns/portrait-cinema.md | MIXTA ENTRA
## Dreamy Underwater Surreal Portrait

### INV-002893 | sec 349 | skills/image/references/patterns/portrait-cinema.md | MIXTA ENTRA
Use for beauty campaigns, conceptual art, or album visuals — a portrait where the subject floats in clear water surrounded by translucent aquatic elements.

### INV-002894 | sec 349 | skills/image/references/patterns/portrait-cinema.md | MIXTA ENTRA
<!-- Source concept: surreal underwater portrait with translucent fish and dreamy caustic light -->

### INV-002895 | sec 349 | skills/image/references/patterns/portrait-cinema.md | MIXTA ENTRA
```
Scene: clear {water_color} water filling the entire frame, caustic light patterns rippling across the subject from above (sunlight through water surface), no visible pool walls or floor — infinite aquatic void
Subject: {person_description}, floating in a relaxed {pose_description}, eyes {eye_state}, hair fanning out in all directions as if weightless, {clothing_description} billowing and suspended in the water
Important Details: {fish_count} translucent {fish_type} swimming in a loose school around the subject — each fish semi-transparent with visible skeletal structure and iridescent scales catching the caustic light, light rays penetrating from above in {light_pattern}, fine air bubbles rising from near the subject's {bubble_source}, fabric of clothing moves independently from the body — folds and hems suspended in mid-drift, skin has a subtle cool {water_tint} cast from the water, overall palette is {palette}, composition framed as a {shot_type}
Use Case: beauty campaign, album cover, conceptual art print, fashion editorial
Constraints: subject's face must be clearly visible and serene (not distressed or holding breath with effort), fish are translucent — not solid opaque tropical fish, no visible water surface edge or pool tiles, no scuba gear or goggles, bubbles are small and delicate not large air pockets, underwater physics must be consistent (everything floats)
```

### INV-002896 | sec 349 | skills/image/references/patterns/portrait-cinema.md | MIXTA ENTRA
**Key levers:** `{water_color}` (deep cerulean #0077B6, pale turquoise #AFEEEE, dark teal #004D4D), `{person_description}`, `{pose_description}` (arms slightly outstretched like a slow free-fall, curled fetal position, one arm reaching upward toward the light), `{eye_state}` (softly closed, open and gazing upward at the light, looking directly at camera), `{clothing_description}` (flowing white silk dress, loose linen shirt and trousers, sheer organza wrap), `{fish_count}` (5-8), `{fish_type}` (jellyfish, small reef fish, elongated glass catfish), `{light_pattern}` (parallel god rays from upper right, scattered dappled caustics, single concentrated beam), `{bubble_source}` (lips, fingertips, fabric edges), `{water_tint}` (blue-green, aquamarine), `{palette}` (teal and ivory, deep blue and gold, seafoam and blush), `{shot_type}` (full body vertical, waist-up centered, three-quarter with negative space below)

### INV-002897 | sec 349 | skills/image/references/patterns/portrait-cinema.md | MIXTA SALE | «Recommended model: NBP»
**Recommended model:** NBP — complex physics (floating hair, fabric, fish transparency, caustic light)

### INV-005729 | sec 667 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/on-screen-text.md | MIXTA ENTRA | composición que reserva espacio al texto
### Negative-space-aware composition

### INV-005730 | sec 667 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/on-screen-text.md | MIXTA ENTRA | composición que reserva espacio al texto
When a shot has on-screen text, the **shot subject must reserve space for it**. This is enforced at storyboard time, not generation time.

### INV-005731 | sec 667 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/on-screen-text.md | MIXTA ENTRA | composición que reserva espacio al texto
If text is in the right third → subject composition leaves the right third clear.
If text is lower third → subject upper two-thirds.
If text is centered → subject framed to negative space around center.

### INV-005732 | sec 667 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/on-screen-text.md | MIXTA SALE | campo rationale de shots.json (storyboard)
This goes into the shot's rationale: "MCU left-third subject, reserves right-third for `text_03`."

### INV-005774 | sec 676 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | tamaño del sujeto en el cuadro

### INV-005784 | sec 676 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | tamaño del sujeto en el cuadro
Default to MCU and MS for talking-head founder content. ECU and CU for product detail and emotion. WS and EWS for context-setting.

### INV-005794 | sec 678 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA SALE | movimiento de cámara: video
## Motion (camera movement)

### INV-005795 | sec 678 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA SALE | movimiento de cámara: video
| Code | Effect | Use when |

### INV-005796 | sec 678 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA SALE | movimiento de cámara: video
|---|---|---|

### INV-005797 | sec 678 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA SALE | movimiento de cámara: video
| `static` | No movement | Default. Static is not boring. |

### INV-005798 | sec 678 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA SALE | movimiento de cámara: video
| `push` | Camera moves toward subject | Building intensity, revealing |

### INV-005799 | sec 678 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA SALE | movimiento de cámara: video
| `pull` | Camera moves away | Releasing, contextualizing |

### INV-005800 | sec 678 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA SALE | movimiento de cámara: video
| `pan-left` / `pan-right` | Camera rotates horizontally | Surveying environment |

### INV-005801 | sec 678 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA SALE | movimiento de cámara: video
| `tilt-up` / `tilt-down` | Camera rotates vertically | Reveal scale or detail |

### INV-005802 | sec 678 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA SALE | movimiento de cámara: video
| `handheld` | Subtle organic shake | Documentary feel, urgency |

### INV-005803 | sec 678 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA SALE | movimiento de cámara: video
| `orbit` | Camera circles subject | Hero shots, product reveal |

### INV-005804 | sec 678 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA SALE | movimiento de cámara: video
| `whip` | Fast pan as transition | Beat-cuts in fast-paced content |

### INV-005805 | sec 678 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | en una imagen fija el movimiento es solo intención para el editor
For AI-generated still frames, motion is mostly intent for the editor. For motion-video prompts (Kling, Veo, Seedance, Hailuo), motion translates directly.

### INV-005813 | sec 680 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | luz definida una vez en series_lock
## Lighting style (referenced from series_lock)

### INV-005814 | sec 680 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | luz definida una vez en series_lock
Don't redefine per shot. Define once in `series_lock.lighting`. Examples:

### INV-005815 | sec 680 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | luz definida una vez en series_lock
- `soft natural side-light, large window left, warm afternoon`

### INV-005816 | sec 680 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | luz definida una vez en series_lock
- `hard top-light, single source, deep shadows, studio black`

### INV-005817 | sec 680 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | luz definida una vez en series_lock
- `practical mixed sources, neon accents, urban night`

### INV-005818 | sec 680 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | luz definida una vez en series_lock
The series_lock string flows into every prompt automatically.

### INV-005819 | sec 681 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | descripción del sujeto
## Subject description

### INV-005820 | sec 681 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | descripción del sujeto
Structured, not poetic. Pattern:

### INV-005821 | sec 681 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | descripción del sujeto
```
[who/what], [doing what], [emotional or compositional note]
```

### INV-005822 | sec 681 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | descripción del sujeto
Good:

### INV-005823 | sec 681 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | descripción del sujeto
```
"Founder, mid-thirties, leaning forward at laptop, face partially turned to window light"
```

### INV-005824 | sec 681 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | descripción del sujeto
Bad:

### INV-005825 | sec 681 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | descripción del sujeto
```
"A determined entrepreneur conquering the digital frontier with passion"
```

### INV-005826 | sec 681 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | descripción del sujeto
Generators reward precision. Adjectives describing emotion ("determined", "passionate") produce stock-photo aesthetics. Describe what the camera actually sees.

### INV-005827 | sec 682 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | qué va en subject, series_lock y brand_lock
## What goes in `subject` vs `series_lock` vs `brand_lock`

### INV-005828 | sec 682 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | qué va en subject, series_lock y brand_lock
This trips people up. The rule:

### INV-005829 | sec 682 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | qué va en subject, series_lock y brand_lock
- **brand_lock**, locked across the entire project. Palette, type, "never" list.

### INV-005830 | sec 682 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | qué va en subject, series_lock y brand_lock
- **series_lock**, locked across this storyboard. Character anchor, environment, lighting style, color grade.

### INV-005831 | sec 682 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | qué va en subject, series_lock y brand_lock
- **subject (per shot)**, what's different about *this* shot. Action, expression, framing-specific composition notes.

### INV-005832 | sec 682 | skills/storyboard-architect/references/shot-grammar.md | MIXTA ENTRA | qué va en subject, series_lock y brand_lock
If you find yourself repeating the same lighting description across shots, it belongs in series_lock. If you find yourself repeating the same color description across storyboards, it belongs in brand_lock.

### INV-007978 | sec 804 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | lo que debe hacer una imagen fija clave
## 1. What an animatic keyframe must do

### INV-007979 | sec 804 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | lo que debe hacer una imagen fija clave
In an animatic the **panel is the unit, not the shot.** A shot has duration, motion, and sound to carry its function across time. A keyframe has one frozen image and must carry the same function in a single glance. So every panel does four things at once, in one still:

### INV-007980 | sec 804 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | lo que debe hacer una imagen fija clave
- **One readable story-beat** — name it in one sentence before generating. If you cannot, the panel is not ready (`dramaturgy.md` §1).

### INV-007981 | sec 804 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | lo que debe hacer una imagen fija clave
- **One function tag** — Establish / Reveal / Power / Pressure / Detail / Reaction / Shift / Impact / Aftermath / Exit (`role-modes.md` §4). The tag is the question the panel answers. In a faceless spot **Reaction** is reassigned from a human face to the *machine's* face — the needle jumping, the slick wrinkling, the nose squatting. Treat gauges and rubber as the actor.

### INV-007982 | sec 804 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | lo que debe hacer una imagen fija clave
- **One emotion** — routed through metal, rubber, light, or anatomy, never named (`dramaturgy.md` §2). Mixed signals = no signal.

### INV-007983 | sec 804 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | lo que debe hacer una imagen fija clave
- **One implied motion** — the movement the still cannot perform, frozen as its physical residue (§4).

### INV-007984 | sec 804 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | lo que debe hacer una imagen fija clave
A panel that carries a beat, a function, an emotion, and an implied motion earns its place. A panel missing any of the four is a *fantik* — wrapper without candy (`dramaturgy.md` §3). The discipline of this whole file is: **you cannot prompt "motion" or "emotion" into a still — you prompt the physical consequence each leaves behind.**

### INV-007985 | sec 804 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | lo que debe hacer una imagen fija clave
---

### INV-007986 | sec 805 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | tarjeta de la imagen fija clave
## 2. The keyframe card

### INV-007987 | sec 805 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | tarjeta de la imagen fija clave
The shot card in `dramaturgy.md` §11 describes a moving shot. A keyframe needs a *still*-specific card: every field describes a frozen image, and three fields are new (implied camera, emotion-via-object, motion-rendered-as-still). Fill every field before generating. Empty fields reveal missing direction.

### INV-007988 | sec 805 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | tarjeta de la imagen fija clave
```text
KEYFRAME CARD
- Panel ID.            01, 02, 03
- Timecode.            on-screen life in the animatic (e.g. 0:10.5, HOLD 2s)
- Function tag.        Establish / Reveal / Power / Pressure / Detail /
                       Reaction / Shift / Impact / Aftermath / Exit
- Beat.                what changes in the story in this one still
- Framing + lens.      ECU / CU / MCU / wide / macro insert / POV + lens token
                       (24mm immersive ... 100mm macro — camera-lighting-vocabulary.md §3)
- Subject / anchor.    the ONE object or anatomy the eye lands on in 0.3s
- Depth layers.        FG job (frame / obstruct / put us inside)
                       MG job (the subject / the act)
                       BG job (stakes / context / rival)
- Implied camera.      the move this still is the first frame OF
                       (push-in, whip, bumper-POV, joust) — what it would do if it ran
- Light + palette.     one motivated source + direction + the surface it rims;
                       concrete colors, amber-ban repeated
- Emotion-via-object.  the feeling a face would show, mapped to an object's state (§5)
- Motion-as-still.     which frozen cue carries the missing movement (§4):
                       smear / partial blur / streak / vector / sharp-blur / held stillness
- Caption / label.     one word: setup / commitment / mistake / recovery /
                       proof-of-speed / result
- Sound.               what audio sits under the held frame, where the cut bites
                       (fill even though it is a still — dramaturgy.md §11)
- Production note.      prop, rig, continuity anchor
```

### INV-007989 | sec 805 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | tarjeta de la imagen fija clave
The card maps straight onto format C: `Function`, `Framing`, `Implied camera`, `Light`, `Sound`, `Emotion-via-object` become the table columns. The three still-specific fields — **implied camera, emotion-via-object, motion-as-still** — are the ones a normal shot card does not force you to name, and they are exactly the ones that keep a panel from going flat.

### INV-007990 | sec 805 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | tarjeta de la imagen fija clave
---

### INV-007991 | sec 806 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | cuántos paneles por beat: planificación del animatic
## 3. How many keyframes per beat — the density ladder

### INV-007992 | sec 806 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | cuántos paneles por beat: planificación del animatic
**Loudness in the audio = panel count in the board.** Silence gets one held panel; a bang gets a burst. This is the still-panel reading of `dramaturgy.md` §12 (rhythm ladder) and `role-modes.md` §3 (editor density: 3-4 beats/5s drama, 4-7 narrative, 6-9 fast montage). The full 30s beat map is the worked board in §8; split it per `patterns-and-genres.md` §3 (30s = 6×5s).

### INV-007993 | sec 806 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | cuántos paneles por beat: planificación del animatic
| Beat | Density | Panels | Why |

### INV-007994 | sec 806 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | cuántos paneles por beat: planificación del animatic
|---|---|---|---|

### INV-007995 | sec 806 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | cuántos paneles por beat: planificación del animatic
| **Ritual / staging** (quiet) | 1 hero panel, held | 1 panel living 1.5-2.5s | A silent beat is monolithic. Do not subdivide it — its power is that nothing moves while the world holds its breath. One symmetric "breath" frame, most negative space, least motion. |

### INV-007996 | sec 806 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | cuántos paneles por beat: planificación del animatic
| **Pressure climbing** (amber stagger) | escalating singles | 3 panels, collapsing length | The Sportsman tree fires three ambers 0.5s apart — three drawable panels, each tighter than the last, cut length collapsing toward the green. |

### INV-007997 | sec 806 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | cuántos paneles por beat: planificación del animatic
| **Launch / impact** (bang) | 3-panel micro-burst → up to 6-9 | 6-9 panels at 8fr → 4fr | The audio bang is rendered as panel *density*, not one big image. Hip-hop montage: bumper-POV smear, side wheelspin, tach flare, clutch hand, lane-stripe strobe. |

### INV-007998 | sec 806 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | cuántos paneles por beat: planificación del animatic
| **Contest** (sustained roar) | alternating | fragment-cluster ↔ one held detail | Never two bang-clusters back to back. Between every burst, one held quiet panel — loud only works next to quiet. |

### INV-007999 | sec 806 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | cuántos paneles por beat: planificación del animatic
| **Aftermath / exit** (cut to silence) | 1 hero panel, held | 1-2 panels, ~2s hold | End on consequence, not explanation. One held still: drifting smoke, hand unclenching, car nosing into dark. |

### INV-008000 | sec 806 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | cuántos paneles por beat: planificación del animatic
**Allocation rule.** A 30s spot lands at ~18-26 panels. Plot the board as a descending duration staircase (≈2.5s → 0.5s → 4fr), broken only by the one pre-launch pause and the one post-finish hold. The board should *read* as an accelerating tachometer even on mute. Give a beat a micro-burst only when the audio detonates; everywhere else, one panel that holds is stronger than three that scatter.

### INV-008001 | sec 806 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | cuántos paneles por beat: planificación del animatic
---

### INV-008002 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
## 4. Composing a still that reads as MOTION

### INV-008003 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
A keyframe cannot ramp, cut, or whip. Each missing move resolves into one of **six frozen cues**. Never write "motion" — write the artifact motion leaves behind. State the cue in the card's *Motion-as-still* field and again in the prompt's camera/light clause; models default to over-clean stabilization and will sand the speed off unless told (§7).

### INV-008004 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
| Cue | What it is in a still | Renders the implied move |

### INV-008005 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
|---|---|---|

### INV-008006 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
| **Directional motion-blur smear** | Background streaked along the travel axis, subject sharp. Guardrail, lane stripes, tree lamps pulled into horizontal light-rods. | tracking pass, POV launch, the whip-pan aftermath |

### INV-008007 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
| **Frozen partial blur** | One moving element blurred (tyre sidewall, smoke bloom, shift hand mid-throw) while the anchor stays crisp — proof of a single arrested instant. | speed-ramp peak, launch, shift |

### INV-008008 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
| **Streak-frame (implied whip)** | The whole panel raked into motion-streaks with one readable shape surviving (a wheel arc, the shifter) — a "between two shots" blur panel. | whip pan, transition beat |

### INV-008009 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
| **Posture / trajectory vector** | A body or car locked in a pose that can only resolve forward — wrist cocked over the shifter, front wheels lifting, weight slung onto the rear axle. The eye finishes the move. | charged pose, freeze frame, launch |

### INV-008010 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
| **Sharp-subject / blurred-field separation** | Shallow-DOF macro: knuckle, needle, latch razor-sharp, everything else dissolved. Isolation = the cut-to-detail frozen. | insert chain, crash-zoom arrival, hip-hop CU |

### INV-008011 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
| **Freeze-frame stillness** | Deliberately *zero* blur, hard edges, held breath — reads as a stopped clock against the blurred panels around it. | the pre-launch held beat, the loaded spring |

### INV-008012 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
Per-technique kit (triad + the still application):

### INV-008013 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
- **Frozen motion blur** — what it does to emotion: differential blur reads as velocity made tactile, the world torn past a steady subject — where seen: Dod Mantle whip-panned every overtake in *Rush* so the background dissolves. — *in a keyframe* [Shift/Impact]: hero car tack-sharp, entire background raked into horizontal streaks, foreground objects elongated into light-rods. The most powerful single-panel speed trick: **sharp hero + fully smeared world.** Equal blur everywhere = soft photo; differential blur = speed.

### INV-008014 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
- **Tyre wrinkle / partial blur** — emotion: physics you can feel, force arrested at its peak — seen: Goodyear's deliberately silent slow-mo of the slick sidewall folding; Ritchie's speed-ramp as the exclamation mark. — *in a keyframe* [Detail/Reaction]: macro into the rear sidewall, the wrinkle caught at its deepest fold, smoke just blooming at the contact patch, everything else crisp. One arrested ten-thousandth of a second. The hero detail to *hold*.

### INV-008015 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
- **Smoke / debris / spray shape** — emotion: force given a visible body, dread made airborne — seen: NHRA burnout-cam, "a wall of smoke" backlit at ground level. — *in a keyframe* [Pressure/Power]: backlit tyre smoke as a luminous wall (front-lit smoke is dead gray nothing); water-box spray frozen as a constellation of specular droplets, each rimmed on its leading edge. Atmosphere only reads when light passes through it (§5).

### INV-008016 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
- **Posture / lean vector** — emotion: a stillness that can only resolve forward; the eye does the launching — seen: Vaughn's charged poses (*Kingsman*, *Layer Cake*) — every frame loaded with a trajectory. — *in a keyframe* [Power/Shift]: **no neutral panels.** Car at idle = front wheels micro-lifting, body slung onto the rear axle. Hand on wheel = fingers mid-clench. Compose the negative space *ahead* of the subject, the direction it is about to fire into.

### INV-008017 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
- **Leading lines / light streak** — emotion: a rail for the eye that also encodes direction and speed — seen: Papamichael's asphalt-in-frame (*Ford v Ferrari*), the road as the speedometer. — *in a keyframe* [Power/Impact]: lane centerline raking to a vanishing point, sodium/mercury practicals smeared as long reflections down wet asphalt. Keep the texture (chevrons, tar seams, rubber marks) so the streak has something to streak.

### INV-008018 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
- **Off-balance dutch** — emotion: imbalance, loss of grip, threat — seen: Raimi's canted attack frames (*Evil Dead II*). — *in a keyframe* [Impact]: cant the horizon hard at the one loss-of-traction beat — tyre breaking away, car stepping sideways. Reserve it for a single instability beat so it stays loud; dutch as wallpaper reads as bad framing.

### INV-008019 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
**The speed-budget rule** (from the race-truth school): a still sells velocity when **at least three of five coexist in one frame** — (a) camera at/below bumper height, (b) asphalt streaking the lower frame, (c) a near foreground reference passing the edge, (d) sharp hero against motion-blurred background, (e) a mechanical-vibration cue (trembling mirror, doubled needle, smeared rim). One cue is a car photo. Three is velocity. If a "fast" panel cannot name three, reframe lower, drag the camera to the asphalt, throw a cone or barrier into the foreground.

### INV-008020 | sec 807 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | movimiento compuesto en una imagen fija
---

### INV-008021 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
## 5. Emotion without faces in a still

### INV-008022 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
A face does four jobs: shows the feeling, shows where it looks, shows the cost, shows the decision. Faces banned, redistribute: **decision → anatomy mid-action; internal state → an object crossing a threshold; looking/rivalry → space** (§6 and `dramaturgy.md` §2). Assign one job per panel and tag it. The emotion must already be frozen *inside the object* — a still cannot let motion blur do the emotional work for it.

### INV-008023 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
Two load-bearing rules:

### INV-008024 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
- **Anatomy only on a decision.** A hand at rest is filler; a hand *deciding* is a shot. Before drawing any hand/foot panel, name the verb — grip / latch / shift / preload / release. No verb, no panel. Render tendons raised, glove leather creasing, one hard side-light carving knuckle texture, the rest in shadow (Leone's hands-before-the-draw; Nike *Take It to the Next Level*, first-person, no hero face).

### INV-008025 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
- **Objects only when they change state.** A static gear-lever says "car stuff" and flatlines. Board the **after** state of a threshold-crossing; the before is implied (Honda *Cog*: every object does something irreversible). Tach pinned in the red, not idling. Ignition lamp blooming live, not dark. Slick caught mid-fold.

### INV-008026 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
Use the substitution table as the core deliverable — when the script wants an emotion and no face is available, render this object at this crop. Cold palette throughout.

### INV-008027 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
| Emotion (a face would show) | Object substitute | Crop | Render note (the still) | Label / tag |

### INV-008028 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
|---|---|---|---|---|

### INV-008029 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
| **Fear / dread** | sweat bead on knuckle; tremor in the mirror reflection (not eyes) | macro / ECU | one hard specular highlight on the bead, cold rim, deep black around it; mirror edge doubled to imply vibration | setup → Pressure |

### INV-008030 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
| **Resolve / commitment** | knuckles whitening on wheel or shifter; foot pinning throttle | CU / MCU | tendons raised, glove leather creasing, grip at maximum; one side-light, cabin in shadow | commitment → Shift |

### INV-008031 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
| **Focus / lock-in** | start-lights reflected in lacquer / headlight glass (not a human eye) | MCU | staging bulbs mirrored as cold points on paint or chrome; tight, centered, still | setup → Detail |

### INV-008032 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
| **Strain (machine at limit)** | tach needle pinned in red arc; slick sidewall wrinkling; exhaust flame | ECU / CU | needle hard against redline, motion-still but unmistakably max; sidewall at deepest fold | proof-of-speed → Pressure |

### INV-008033 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
| **Mistake / panic** | tyre shake (sidewall "goes square"), wheel kicking back, revs over-flared | CU | sidewall deformed wrong, blur direction fighting the lane; tach spiked past the band | mistake → Impact |

### INV-008034 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
| **Triumph / release** | grip relaxing; chute blooming; one nose past the stripe; smoke through headlight beams | CU → WS | fingers unfurling off the wheel; or distant clean geometry of the win, cold backlight through smoke | result → Aftermath / Exit |

### INV-008035 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
| **Anticipation (pre-launch)** | finger over ignition / line-lock; pre-stage bulb lit; held shifter | ECU / CU | finger a hair off the button, bulb just lit cold, everything frozen — the "silence" panel | setup → Pressure |

### INV-008036 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
State-change cheat-sheet (board the live state; the dead is implied): ignition dark → lamp blooming; tach idle → needle pinned red; slick round → wrinkle folded deep; water-box mirror-still → shredded by launch; front wheels planted → lifting; chassis level → squatted; chute packed → bloomed at the trap.

### INV-008037 | sec 808 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | emoción sin rostro en una imagen fija
---

### INV-008038 | sec 809 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el cuadro cargado: capas de profundidad y foco
## 6. The loaded frame for one panel

### INV-008039 | sec 809 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el cuadro cargado: capas de profundidad y foco
A keyframe is a diagram the viewer solves in 0.3s and re-reads with pleasure at 1s. With no face, the geometry and the depth stack do the acting.

### INV-008040 | sec 809 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el cuadro cargado: capas de profundidad y foco
> **Core law of the loaded panel.** One focal point, one emotion, three working depth layers, zero dead objects. Answer one question in the first glance; reward a second look.

### INV-008041 | sec 809 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el cuadro cargado: capas de profundidad y foco
**Depth-layer jobs (FG / MG / BG).** Write the prompt in three explicit depth clauses, each with a stated job. This is the engine of "reads in one glance, rewards a second" (extends `dramaturgy.md` §10 into a single still). If all three planes carry the same blur or scale, you have one plane, not three — force a sharp MG subject against a soft FG frame and a soft BG stake (shallow DOF is the panel's hierarchy tool, `camera-lighting-vocabulary.md` §3).

### INV-008042 | sec 809 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el cuadro cargado: capas de profundidad y foco
| Plane | Default job | Drag-race objects | Function it serves |

### INV-008043 | sec 809 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el cuadro cargado: capas de profundidad y foco
|---|---|---|---|

### INV-008044 | sec 809 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el cuadro cargado: capas de profundidad y foco
| **Foreground** | frame / obstruct / put us inside | roll-cage bar, mirror edge, harness strap, smoke wisp, wet glass | Pressure, Power |

### INV-008045 | sec 809 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el cuadro cargado: capas de profundidad y foco
| **Midground** | the subject / the act | hand on shifter, knuckles on wheel, foot on pedal, tach face | Detail, Shift, Impact |

### INV-008046 | sec 809 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el cuadro cargado: capas de profundidad y foco
| **Background** | stakes / context / rival | staging bulb, rival lane headlight, finish line, narrowing barrier | Establish, Reveal, Pressure |

### INV-008047 | sec 809 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el cuadro cargado: capas de profundidad y foco
**One-glance + reward** — what it does to emotion: an instantly readable frame creates trust; a frame that rewards re-reading creates density and worth. With no face to anchor on, a muddy panel collapses to nothing. — *in a keyframe*: name the single focal point before generating (tach needle / staging bulb / knuckle), put it on a strong line, demote everything else to support. The second-look reward is a reflection, a bridge object, or a state indicator — one, not a clutter.

### INV-008048 | sec 809 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el cuadro cargado: capas de profundidad y foco
**Centerline spine, then break symmetry** — emotion: a single dominant axis (lane stripe, the Christmas Tree, a dividing wall) gives the eye a rail and makes even a chaotic frame readable; breaking it the instant one car noses ahead reads as momentum. — seen: Anderson centers for instant legibility; Bong breaks balanced staging to show a power shift. — *in a keyframe*: build one head-on symmetric "breath" panel — both cars mirrored across the centerline, Tree dead-middle, equal negative space (tag **Establish/Pressure**, hold longest). On the next panel, break the mirror — hero a nose ahead, rival shoved soft, centerline slid off the third (tag **Shift/Impact**). Use the pair exactly once, at the launch; overusing symmetry kills the speed.

### INV-008049 | sec 809 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el cuadro cargado: capas de profundidad y foco
**Functional vs decorative clutter — the evidence test.** Keep an object only if it does at least one of: (1) **indicator of state** (replaces the face — tach in the red, white knuckles), (2) **stage of action** (advances the beat — front wheel 7" off the line = pre-stage), (3) **bridge to the next panel** (sets up a match-cut or a reach). Dead detail — coffee cup, mirror dice, dashboard sticker — shows no state, advances nothing, bridges nowhere. Cut it. "Each object is a clue" (Park); functional clutter *loads* the frame, decorative clutter *weakens* it.

### INV-008050 | sec 809 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el cuadro cargado: capas de profundidad y foco
**Angle encodes the beat** (no face, so the angle *is* the emotion): low / ground-level = power, threat, launch force (**Power, Impact**); eye-level lateral = parity, rivalry stated (**Establish**); high / top-down = judgment, smallness, aftermath (**Aftermath, Exit**).

### INV-008051 | sec 809 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el cuadro cargado: capas de profundidad y foco
---

### INV-008052 | sec 810 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | de la tarjeta al prompt de imagen
## 7. From keyframe to image-gen prompt

### INV-008053 | sec 810 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | de la tarjeta al prompt de imagen
A keyframe card becomes a still prompt by following the universal skeleton (`universal-rules.md` U1) with weight at the start (U2). **Lead with framing + lens + light + palette + anchor object** — these are the load-bearing tokens for a still; the model puts most attention on the first 30-40% of tokens.

### INV-008054 | sec 810 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | de la tarjeta al prompt de imagen
Panel → prompt skeleton:

### INV-008055 | sec 810 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | de la tarjeta al prompt de imagen
```text
[Framing + lens]           e.g. "Low bumper-height macro, 24mm, shot past a blurred roll-cage bar"
[Anchor subject + state]   the ONE object at its threshold: "rear slick sidewall wrinkling at the contact patch"
[Depth clauses, 3 jobs]    FG frame / obstruct · MG subject / act · BG stake / rival
[Motion-as-still cue]      "sharp subject against horizontal motion-blurred background, foreground reference elongated into a light-streak, rear rim spokes smeared to a disc"
[Light + palette]          one motivated source + direction + surface it rims; concrete colors; amber-ban
[Texture]                  "fine film grain heaviest in shadow, halation on the practicals, one anamorphic flare, crushed blacks"
[Face rule]                "no face" — or "driver a featureless silhouette against blown rival headlights, only knuckle highlights legible"
[Aspect / no-speed-up note]  "no speed-ramp; build velocity into the static frame"
```

### INV-008056 | sec 810 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | de la tarjeta al prompt de imagen
Rules that keep the still honest:

### INV-008057 | sec 810 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | de la tarjeta al prompt de imagen
- **Name the differential blur explicitly.** Models default to over-clean stabilization. Write "motion blur on background, fast-shutter look, sharp subject, rim spokes smeared to a disc." Equal/no blur is the most-faked and most-failed cue (anti-fake firewall, §4 speed-budget).

### INV-008058 | sec 810 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | de la tarjeta al prompt de imagen
- **Keep faces out or fragmented.** Convert any would-be face to silhouette / contre-jour with a rim and one glint. No features.

### INV-008059 | sec 810 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | de la tarjeta al prompt de imagen
- **Render audio as physical consequence, never as a named sound.** "Loud engine" is banned filler (`dramaturgy.md` §2) — prompt "heat-haze warp off the headers, exhaust flame, chassis squat, sidewall wrinkle" instead.

### INV-008060 | sec 810 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | de la tarjeta al prompt de imagen
- **Anti-sterile guard.** If the panel could be captioned "clean," "beautiful lighting," or "high quality," it has failed — name the grain, the bloom, the crushed black, the one specular hit (`universal-rules.md` U12). One motivated source dominates; ~70% of the frame stays unlit (`camera-lighting-vocabulary.md` §5-7).

### INV-008061 | sec 810 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | la primera frase (vocabulario de encuadre, lente y luz) es de imagen; el resto entrega a video
Cross-link the vocabulary file for every framing/lens/light token. Then **the keyframe is the locked first frame**; hand it to `seedance.md` / `kling.md` / `veo.md` with the single dominant move (one per 5s — `camera-lighting-vocabulary.md` §2) to animate it. Name in the model syntax: "low bumper-height camera, asphalt streaking, motion blur on background, sharp subject, no speed-up."

### INV-008062 | sec 810 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | de la tarjeta al prompt de imagen
---

### INV-008063 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
## 8. Worked keyframe board for a 30s race spot

### INV-008064 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
A Sportsman-tree drag run, cold palette, no faces — ~13 panels using the card. This is the deliverable template the user fills: each row is a compressed keyframe card, format-C compatible (`Time | Shot | Function | Action | Camera | Light | Sound | Emotion`). Cut length collapses toward the green, holds on the wrinkle, then accelerates to the finish.

### INV-008065 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
| Time | Shot (anchor) | Function | Action (state-change + motion-as-still) | Camera (implied move) | Light (cold) | Sound | Emotion-via-object |

### INV-008066 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

### INV-008067 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
| 0.0–2.5 | Slick into water box | Establish | Front tyre breaks mirror-still water; bulb reflected in the sheet | locked macro, low (held) | steel-blue, wet specular | idle lope, water trickle | coiled stillness |

### INV-008068 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
| 2.5–5.0 | Burnout wall | Power | Rear erupts a smoke wall, car straining on line-lock; smoke backlit luminous | ground-level wide (burnout-cam) | cyan backlight through smoke, red tail bleed | lope → tyre scream | leashed force |

### INV-008069 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
| 5.0–6.5 | Pre-stage bulb + front tyre | Pressure | Tyre creeps, top blue bulb lights, beam line as graphic spine | CU, static | blue glow on wet metal | lope thickens | held breath |

### INV-008070 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
| 6.5–8.0 | Stage bulb + knuckles | Pressure | Second bulb lit, knuckles whiten on the trans-brake (depth: bulb BG, hand FG) | CU, depth layers, static | two blue bulbs, crushed black | idle DROPS to silence | point of no return |

### INV-008071 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
| 8.0–9.5 | Amber stagger (1 of 3) | Pressure → Shift | First amber lights; foot pre-loading the pedal | tree-cam, snap-tight | amber pulse (sole warm, toxic) | vacuum of silence | trigger tension |

### INV-008072 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
| 9.5–10.3 | Held breath frame | Pressure | Staged car dead-still, last amber, tach needle the only tremor | symmetric, locked (HOLD) | amber flare, steel rim | silence (the pause) | loaded spring |

### INV-008073 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
| 10.3–11.6 | LAUNCH lunge | Impact | Both cars squat, front wheels lift, green flares, asphalt smears back | low bumper-POV, fisheye (smear) | green flash + hard flare | BANG, leads pic 3fr | release / violence |

### INV-008074 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
| 11.6–13.5 | **Slick wrinkle** (HOLD) | Detail / Reaction | Sidewall folds and hooks at the contact patch; speed-ramp pause, partial blur | macro slick-cam (frozen ramp) | hard side light, smoke bloom | tyre bite (near-silent) | physics felt |

### INV-008075 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
| 13.5–15.5 | Cabin shake | Pressure | Needle buried in red, mirror doubled by vibration, glass strobing | hard-mount interior (tremor) | strobing exterior, red needle accent | rising rev, gear stab | strain |

### INV-008076 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
| 15.5–18.5 | Bumper POV | Pressure | Asphalt tears under the nose, lane stripes strobe to a vanishing point | low forward hood-cam, 24mm (radial smear) | streaked specular, mercury-white | doppler build | commitment |

### INV-008077 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
| 18.5–22.5 | Joust / parallel pass | Power | Rival closes head-on, offset; cone huge-blurred on the opposite edge | chase tracking + joust (closing) | streaked roadside lights, red tail accent | engine duel, doppler swell | rivalry |

### INV-008078 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
| 22.5–26.5 | Finish | Impact | Slicks blown-up and distorted, two cars inches apart across the line | slight high, finish-cam | cold-white finish pool | sound peak → cut | margin |

### INV-008079 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
| 26.5–30.0 | Hand unclenches / chute | Aftermath / Exit | Fingers release the wheel; chute bloomed, smoke drifting through a beam | ECU then rear wide (settling) | single cold rim, residue haze | hard cut to silence, one breath | consequence / release |

### INV-008080 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard
Notes on the board: the 9.5–10.3 held panel is the single most-held still — the pause that triggers the bang. The launch is *not* one hero frame; in a fuller cut, fan 10.3–11.6 into a 6-9 panel micro-burst (§3) — that expansion is what carries the 13 compressed rows up to the ~18-26 panel count of §3. The wrinkle is the one place time expands inside the loud section. No two bang-clusters touch; every burst is separated by a held quiet panel. End on consequence, held ~2s — the vacuum is the punctuation.

### INV-008081 | sec 811 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | tablero de animatic de 30 s (tiempo, sonido, densidad): storyboard

### INV-008082 | sec 812 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | checklist de la imagen fija
## 9. Checklist: does this keyframe earn its place

### INV-008083 | sec 812 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | checklist de la imagen fija
Run before every panel ships. Drop the panel if it fails.

### INV-008084 | sec 812 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | checklist de la imagen fija
- [ ] **Function tag named.** One of Establish / Reveal / Power / Pressure / Detail / Reaction / Shift / Impact / Aftermath / Exit. No tag → drop it.

### INV-008085 | sec 812 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | checklist de la imagen fija
- [ ] **One readable beat**, sayable in one sentence. If you cannot caption it (setup / commitment / mistake / recovery / proof-of-speed / result), it is a *fantik* — drop it.

### INV-008086 | sec 812 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | checklist de la imagen fija
- [ ] **Emotion-via-object** present — the feeling a face would carry, frozen in a knuckle / needle / pedal / smoke / sweat-bead (§5). No state-change, no emotion → drop it.

### INV-008087 | sec 812 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | checklist de la imagen fija
- [ ] **Speed or drama cue** present — ≥1 of the six motion-as-still cues (smear / partial blur / streak / vector / sharp-blur / held stillness), or a deliberate held-still pause. "Fast" panels carry ≥3 of the five speed cues (§4).

### INV-008088 | sec 812 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | checklist de la imagen fija
- [ ] **State-change, not static fetish** — the object crosses a threshold (idle→flare, clean→smoked, planted→lifting). No idle anatomy, no parked gear-lever.

### INV-008089 | sec 812 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | checklist de la imagen fija
- [ ] **Three depth layers**, each with a stated job (FG frame/obstruct, MG subject, BG stake). One focal point; every other object is evidence, not decoration.

### INV-008090 | sec 812 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | checklist de la imagen fija
- [ ] **Implied camera named** — the move this still is the first frame of, physically mountable on the car (no god-angle render).

### INV-008091 | sec 812 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | checklist de la imagen fija
- [ ] **Cold palette held** — steel/cyan/magenta/mercury-white, red and green as accents only, amber ≤15% and point-source (toxic, never cozy). One motivated source, ~70% unlit, one hard specular hit. Texture named (grain, halation, one flare), not "clean."

### INV-008092 | sec 812 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | checklist de la imagen fija
- [ ] **No face**, or a face fragmented past usefulness (silhouette / reflection only).

### INV-008093 | sec 812 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | celda de sonido de la tarjeta (storyboard)
- [ ] **Sound cell filled** even though the panel is a still — what audio underlies the held frame, where the cut bites (`dramaturgy.md` §11).

### INV-008094 | sec 812 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | densidad de paneles (storyboard)
- [ ] **Earns its density slot** — a held beat gets one panel, a bang gets a burst; the board reads as an accelerating tachometer on mute (§3).

### INV-008095 | sec 812 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA SALE | entrega al modelo de video como primer frame
If a panel survives all ten, it is an *opornyi kadr*, not wallpaper. Hand it to `seedance.md` / `kling.md` / `veo.md` as the locked first frame.

### INV-008096 | sec 812 | skills/video/references/animatic-keyframes.md | MIXTA ENTRA | checklist de la imagen fija
---

### INV-008198 | sec 820 | skills/video/references/camera-lighting-vocabulary.md | MIXTA ENTRA | calidad de luz

### INV-008215 | sec 821 | skills/video/references/camera-lighting-vocabulary.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta con colores concretos
## 7. Color discipline

### INV-008216 | sec 821 | skills/video/references/camera-lighting-vocabulary.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta con colores concretos
Define palette with concrete colors. Never write "cinematic colors."

### INV-008217 | sec 821 | skills/video/references/camera-lighting-vocabulary.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta con colores concretos
Bad. "cinematic colors."
Good. "Cold blue-gray shadows, desaturated skin tones, greenish fridge spill, black negative space, no warm yellow tones."

### INV-008218 | sec 821 | skills/video/references/camera-lighting-vocabulary.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta con colores concretos
If the user bans a color, obey it strictly. Repeat the ban in every clip of a multi-clip sequence.

### INV-008219 | sec 821 | skills/video/references/camera-lighting-vocabulary.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta con colores concretos
Common palettes.

### INV-008220 | sec 821 | skills/video/references/camera-lighting-vocabulary.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta con colores concretos
- Fincher cold. Cold blue-gray shadows, desaturated skin, black negative space.

### INV-008221 | sec 821 | skills/video/references/camera-lighting-vocabulary.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta con colores concretos
- Deakins natural. Warm amber interiors, cool blue exteriors, clean contrast.

### INV-008222 | sec 821 | skills/video/references/camera-lighting-vocabulary.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta con colores concretos
- Wong Kar-wai. Saturated warm reds, deep greens, hazy practicals.

### INV-008223 | sec 821 | skills/video/references/camera-lighting-vocabulary.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta con colores concretos
- Glazer neon. Black, one dominant neon hue (magenta or teal), hard edges.

### INV-008224 | sec 821 | skills/video/references/camera-lighting-vocabulary.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta con colores concretos
- Commercial creamy. Warm creams, soft pastels, clean whites, no harsh blacks.

### INV-008225 | sec 821 | skills/video/references/camera-lighting-vocabulary.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta con colores concretos
- Safdie chaotic. Mixed sources, overlapping color temperatures, urban neon spill.

### INV-008571 | sec 867 | skills/video/references/fixes-and-skeletons.md | MIXTA SALE | manos: negativos para Kling y frase positiva para Seedance y Veo

### Twins / face-blending in multi-person scenes

### INV-008586 | sec 871 | skills/video/references/fixes-and-skeletons.md | MIXTA ENTRA | gemelos y caras fundidas en escenas con varias personas
Two or more characters converge into near-identical faces, or background extras clone the hero.

### INV-008587 | sec 871 | skills/video/references/fixes-and-skeletons.md | MIXTA ENTRA | gemelos y caras fundidas en escenas con varias personas


### INV-008982 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro
## 3. Selling speed in a single frame

### INV-008983 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro
A still has no engine note and zero motion. It sells velocity through the **speed-budget rule** — a frame reads fast only when **≥3 of five cues coexist** (camera at/below bumper height · asphalt streaking the lower frame · a near foreground reference passing the edge · sharp hero against motion-blurred background · a mechanical-vibration cue). The full rule, with the anti-fake firewall, lives in `animatic-keyframes.md` §4; do not re-derive it. One cue is a car photo; three is velocity; five and even a parked car reads dangerous. If a "fast" panel can't name three, reframe lower, drag the camera to the asphalt, throw a barrier into the foreground.

### INV-008984 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro
Two race-domain extensions the general file does not carry:

### INV-008985 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro
**The foreground reference catalog** (cue *c*, the cheapest and strongest speed multiplier). Place the reference *near the lens* (so it smears most) and *passing the edge* (so it reads as transiting, not parked). Keep one near object tack-sharp so the brain measures the smear against it.

### INV-008986 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro
| Reference object | Where to place it | Speed it implies |

### INV-008987 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro
|---|---|---|

### INV-008988 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro
| Cone / timing block | near edge, elongated/smeared | lane-level rush |

### INV-008989 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro
| Armco barrier / wall | scraping the near side, streaked | proximity + velocity |

### INV-008990 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro
| Lane stripe / chevron | lower frame, smeared toward bottom | ground speed |

### INV-008991 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro
| Christmas-Tree pylon | foreground, sharp anchor; car blurred | launch reference + timer |

### INV-008992 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro
| Tree / lamp post / grandstand | whipping past, motion-rod | open-road pace |

### INV-008993 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro
| Tire smoke / spray | raked by backlight across frame | force + air movement |

### INV-008994 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro
| Marshal / crowd at periphery | blurred, fragmented | scale + danger |

### INV-008995 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro
**Long-lens compression** — the second speed register and the antidote to "only blur sells speed". Frankenheimer's 1000mm stacked distant cars onto the hero so closing distance reads as collapse. Pair compression panels with low-wide panels in the cut; never board the whole spot on one lens. In `seedance.md`/`veo.md` syntax name "motion blur, fast-shutter look on background, sharp subject" explicitly — models default to over-clean stabilization (the prompt skeleton is `animatic-keyframes.md` §7).

### INV-008996 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro
(The full "what kills the speed" audit lives once, in §10.)

### INV-008997 | sec 957 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | velocidad en un solo cuadro

### INV-009054 | sec 962 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | luz y color sin rostro
## 7. Light & color for speed without faces

### INV-009055 | sec 962 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | luz y color sin rostro
Faces are banned, so light is the only actor left. It does not "set a mood" — it **rims a material** so a piece of metal, rubber or skin reports the emotion a face would. General light grammar (sources, direction, quality) is `camera-lighting-vocabulary.md` §4–7; the anti-sterile firewall is `animatic-keyframes.md` §7 + `universal-rules.md` U12. The race-specific governing law: *embrace the dark* (crush blacks, ~70% of the frame unlit), *one motivated practical per panel* (Tree lamp, headlight, sodium lamp, brake glow), *light is specular not ambient* (every panel needs ≥1 hard specular hit — no specular, no information).

### INV-009056 | sec 963 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta fría
### The cold combative palette

### INV-009057 | sec 963 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta fría
| Color | Emotional job | Where it lives in the keyframe |

### INV-009058 | sec 963 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta fría
|---|---|---|

### INV-009059 | sec 963 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta fría
| Hard white / dirty mercury white | clinical pressure, the white-hot edge of commitment | top/side key on knuckles, metal lip; blown highlight on chrome |

### INV-009060 | sec 963 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta fría
| Cyan / electric teal | cold adrenaline, machine-alive | backlight through burnout smoke; spill on wet asphalt; rim on rear-quarter |

### INV-009061 | sec 963 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta fría
| Steel blue | night base, isolation, the void | negative space, deep shadow body, the unlit 70% |

### INV-009062 | sec 963 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta fría
| Magenta / cold violet | aggression, neon menace | practical sign spill on roofline; reflection ribbon down wet lane |

### INV-009063 | sec 963 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta fría
| **Red (accent only)** | danger, redline, point of no return | brake disc glow, tail-lights, Tree bulbs in lacquer, tach needle in the red |

### INV-009064 | sec 963 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta fría
| Green (accent only) | release, GO | the Tree's green bulb, its single reflection in a headlight lens or paint |

### INV-009065 | sec 963 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta fría
| Toxic amber/sodium (rationed) | temporary, contaminated, alien — never cozy | sodium lamp as a passing smear; spilled fluid catching one lamp; the Tree's three ambers as countdown only |

### INV-009066 | sec 963 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | paleta fría
**Warmth as toxin** is the only permitted heat: brake-disc/exhaust glow (the machine at its thermal limit), a header flame stab gone in a frame, the Tree's alien ambers, a sickly sodium smear. Any warm element must be **point-source and surrounded by cold**. If amber fills more than ~15% of the frame it has become a wash — kill it.

### INV-009067 | sec 964 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | recetas de luz
### Concrete lighting recipes (drop-in for the format-C Light column: source · direction · quality · what it rims)

### INV-009068 | sec 964 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | recetas de luz
- **Hard key on the working hand** [Detail/Pressure]: single hard source 45° camera-side onto knuckles on the shifter — finger shadows rake the skin, sweat reads as discrete specular dots, veins as ridges, background crushed. The whitening knuckle IS the fear.

### INV-009069 | sec 964 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | recetas de luz
- **Backlight / rim on metal edge** [Power]: hard white or cyan source behind/above at low angle drawing one bright line down the roof gutter, mirror housing or wing edge; body in shadow, only the contour glows, against black sky.

### INV-009070 | sec 964 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | recetas de luz
- **Backlit smoke** [Pressure/Establish]: hard cyan or mercury-white punching *through* tire smoke from behind; volumetric shafts, the car a dark mass dissolving into glowing haze, red tail-light bleed inside the smoke for the danger accent. Front-lit smoke is dead gray nothing — always rim it from behind.

### INV-009071 | sec 964 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | recetas de luz
- **Brake-disc glow** [Detail/Impact]: macro on the wheel, glowing orange-red rotor through the spokes as the *only* warm element (toxic heat), rim the caliper and spokes with that glow, everything else steel blue — the one ember in a cold frame.

### INV-009072 | sec 964 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | recetas de luz
- **Specular on wet asphalt — the mirror** [Establish/Shift]: low bumper-height, lane stretching away, practicals (Tree, sodium lamps, headlights) reflected as long vertical smears down the wet surface as the composition's spine; pull the real lamps half out of frame, keep the mirror. Wet asphalt at night = `dramaturgy.md` §9 guilt/threat, and the cheapest way to put more cold light in frame without lifting blacks.

### INV-009073 | sec 964 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | recetas de luz
- **Silhouette / contre-jour** [Power/Pressure]: helmet/shoulder as pure black shape against a blown headlight or the Tree's amber bank, one thin rim defining the form plus one glint on the visor edge, no features. The brain fills the dark with something worse than any face.

### INV-009074 | sec 964 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | recetas de luz
- **The Christmas Tree as the scene's lamp and clock** [Pressure→Impact]: put the Tree bank in frame as the dominant practical — its glow the only key. Stage 1: two blue bulbs as cold points in the lacquer. Stage 2: three ambers blazing, amber-as-toxin spill on wet asphalt, held. Stage 3: green pops, a single green reflection streaks the hood. Match-cut the round bulb → round headlight → round tach.

### INV-009075 | sec 964 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | recetas de luz
**Texture and atmosphere.** Name the grain, never "clean": fine-to-medium film grain (heaviest in shadow), halation/bloom on hard practicals (soft glow ring + hard hot core), one anamorphic flare off the dominant source, slight chromatic fringe on the hottest edges (anti-sterile test in `animatic-keyframes.md` §7). One atmospheric pressure per panel (`dramaturgy.md` §9, Kurosawa) — backlit tire smoke, frozen launch grit lit on its leading edge, heat-haze warping the rimmed edge, wet asphalt as the mirror. Don't stack smoke + haze + spray in one still or it turns to mud. Keep Tony Scott's restlessness and grain; discard his warm grade.

### INV-009076 | sec 964 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | recetas de luz
---

### INV-009098 | sec 967 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | anti-fake del cuadro
## 10. Anti-fake guard

### INV-009099 | sec 967 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | anti-fake del cuadro
The race-specific list of panels that kill credibility — do not draw. (The general per-panel earns-its-place checklist is `animatic-keyframes.md` §9; run that on every panel as well.)

### INV-009100 | sec 967 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | anti-fake del cuadro
- [ ] No high, clean, "beauty" god-angle for action beats — drop to bumper height; low/wide/close beats polish.

### INV-009101 | sec 967 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | anti-fake del cuadro
- [ ] No perfectly stable cabin — render visible vibration (trembling mirror, doubled needle, smeared rim); tilt 1–3°.

### INV-009102 | sec 967 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | anti-fake del cuadro
- [ ] No long-lens speed panel without road texture or a passing reference object.

### INV-009103 | sec 967 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | anti-fake del cuadro
- [ ] No panel that only works "when sped up" — build the speed into the static composition (squat, lifted nose, slick wrinkle).

### INV-009104 | sec 967 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | anti-fake del cuadro
- [ ] No air around the car on a speed beat — crowd the near edge with barrier/rival/wall.

### INV-009105 | sec 967 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | anti-fake del cuadro
- [ ] No sharp wheel rim on a moving car — smear the spokes into a disc.

### INV-009106 | sec 967 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | anti-fake del cuadro
- [ ] No shiny-bodywork beauty pass — shoot force transmission (tire load, chassis squat, exhaust flame).

### INV-009107 | sec 967 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA SALE | orden de los beats rituales de la secuencia
- [ ] No ritual beat out of order or skipped (§5) — a knowing viewer reads the fake instantly.

### INV-009108 | sec 967 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | anti-fake del cuadro
- [ ] No warm cozy grade — cold palette, red as danger accent only, amber ≤15% and toxic (§7).

### INV-009109 | sec 967 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | anti-fake del cuadro
- [ ] No banned panel: static fetish insert (lever/badge/wheel with no verb, no state-change); anatomy at rest; a detail you cannot caption with a §4 label; all-proof-of-speed board with no commitment/mistake/recovery; decorative dashboard clutter (keyring, dice, coffee cup — "each object is a clue"); warm wash without narrative function.

### INV-009110 | sec 967 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | anti-fake del cuadro
Before sending the board, run `animatic-keyframes.md` §9 (per-panel master checklist), `dramaturgy.md` §15 and `universal-rules.md` §13 over it. The most-violated step is the three-detail check — do not skip it.

### INV-009111 | sec 967 | skills/video/references/race-and-speed.md | MIXTA ENTRA | anti-fake del cuadro

### INV-011159 | sec 1183 | skills/visual-media/SKILL.md | MIXTA ENTRA | triángulo de exposición
### Triángulo de exposición

### INV-011160 | sec 1183 | skills/visual-media/SKILL.md | MIXTA ENTRA | triángulo de exposición
```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Triángulo de exposición — Exposure Triangle                    │
│                                                                 │
│              Diafragma / Aperture                               │
│                 /\                                              │
│                /  \                                             │
│               /    \                                            │
│              /      \                                           │
│             /        \                                          │
│            /          \                                         │
│           /            \                                        │
│          /______________\                                       │
│    Obturador Shutter    ISO Sensibilidad                        │
│                                                                 │
│  Diafragma (f/1.4 – f/22)                                       │
│  ├─ Abierto (número bajo): poca profundidad, fondo desenfocado  │
│  └─ Cerrado (número alto): mucha profundidad, todo nítido       │
│                                                                 │
│  Obturador (1/4000s – 30s)                                      │
│  ├─ Alta velocidad: congela el movimiento                       │
│  └─ Baja velocidad: motion blur, estelas de luz                 │
│                                                                 │
│  ISO (100 – 12800+)                                             │
│  ├─ ISO bajo: imagen limpia y detallada                         │
│  └─ ISO alto: sirve en poca luz, pero introduce ruido           │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### INV-011161 | sec 1184 | skills/visual-media/SKILL.md | MIXTA ENTRA | reglas de composición
### Reglas de composición

### INV-011162 | sec 1184 | skills/visual-media/SKILL.md | MIXTA ENTRA | reglas de composición
```markdown
## Composición básica

### Regla de tercios
- Divide el encuadre en una retícula de 3×3
- Coloca el sujeto en los puntos de intersección
- Sitúa el horizonte en la línea superior o inferior

### Líneas guía
- Usa líneas para conducir la mirada
- Caminos, ríos, barandales
- Que apunten al sujeto

### Encuadre dentro del encuadre
- Enmarca al sujeto con elementos del primer plano
- Marcos de puerta, ventanas, ramas
- Aporta capas y profundidad

### Simetría y equilibrio
- Simetría perfecta = estabilidad, solemnidad
- Asimetría = dinamismo, tensión
- Equilibra el peso visual

### Espacio negativo
- El vacío refuerza el punto focal
- Da "espacio para respirar" al sujeto
- Estética minimalista
```

### INV-011163 | sec 1185 | skills/visual-media/SKILL.md | MIXTA ENTRA | uso de la luz
### Uso de la luz

### INV-011164 | sec 1185 | skills/visual-media/SKILL.md | MIXTA ENTRA | uso de la luz
| Tipo de luz | Característica | Ideal para |

### INV-011165 | sec 1185 | skills/visual-media/SKILL.md | MIXTA ENTRA | uso de la luz
