### INV-012171 | sec 1337 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA | ejemplo de calibración entre adaptadores, GPT Image incluido
# One Shot, All Adapters

### INV-012172 | sec 1337 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA | ejemplo de calibración entre adaptadores, GPT Image incluido
This example takes a single shot from the WhyStrohm 30-second pitch and renders it through the generator adapters in the pack. Use it as a calibration reference: same intent, same data, different prompt syntaxes. Six stills adapters are shown, plus the default motion adapter (Kling) standing in for the motion video lane.

### INV-012173 | sec 1338 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA | shot de origen del ejemplo
## The source shot

### INV-012174 | sec 1338 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA | shot de origen del ejemplo
From `examples/30s-pain-proof-promise/shots.json`:

### INV-012175 | sec 1338 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA | shot de origen del ejemplo
```json
{
  "id": "shot_04",
  "beat": "reframe",
  "start": 11.0,
  "end": 16.0,
  "framing": "MCU",
  "angle": "eye-level",
  "motion": "push",
  "depth_of_field": "shallow",
  "subject": "founder, same posture, looking at camera, expression shifts from tired to clear, small almost-smile",
  "rationale": "Slow push as the reframe lands. Same character, same environment, only the expression changes. The change is the point."
}
```

### INV-012176 | sec 1339 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA | series_lock del ejemplo
## Series lock context (constant across all adapters)

### INV-012177 | sec 1339 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA | series_lock del ejemplo
```
character: founder, mid-thirties, salt-and-pepper hair, navy crewneck, calm posture
environment: minimalist home office, white walls, oak desk, single houseplant
lighting: soft natural side-light, large window camera-left, warm afternoon golden hour
color_grade: warm filmic, muted teal shadows, slight grain
```

### INV-012178 | sec 1340 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA | brand mood del ejemplo
## Brand mood

### INV-012179 | sec 1340 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA | brand mood del ejemplo
`calm, considered, operator (not creator), confident without volume`

### INV-012180 | sec 1341 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA
## Outputs

Each `.txt` file in this directory is the prompt for one adapter:

- `midjourney.txt`. Midjourney v7

### INV-012184 | sec 1341 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA SALE | salida de otro modelo (o de gpt-image-1.5, ver sección 1344)

### INV-012186 | sec 1341 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA SALE | salida de otro modelo (o de gpt-image-1.5, ver sección 1344)

### INV-012187 | sec 1341 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA SALE | salida de otro modelo (o de gpt-image-1.5, ver sección 1344)
- `seedream.txt`. Seedream 4.5

### INV-012188 | sec 1341 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA SALE | salida de otro modelo (o de gpt-image-1.5, ver sección 1344)
- `kling.txt`. Kling 3.0 (motion video, the default motion adapter)

### INV-012189 | sec 1341 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA SALE | adaptadores de video
The other three motion adapters (`veo` for dialogue/lipsync, `seedance` for multi-shot sequences, `hailuo` for budget iteration) follow the same five-layer anatomy as Kling and are chosen by need; see their files in `adapters/`. This single silent push shot doesn't exercise dialogue or a sequence, so Kling is the representative motion example here.

### INV-012190 | sec 1341 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA
Note the differences:

### INV-012191 | sec 1341 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA SALE | sintaxis de Midjourney
- **Midjourney** is a comma-separated stack with `--ar`, `--style raw`, `--s 50` flags

### INV-012192 | sec 1341 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA SALE | sintaxis de Flux
- **Flux** is natural-language paragraphs with no flags, parameters live in the API call

### INV-012193 | sec 1341 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA
- **GPT Image** uses paragraph breaks and explicit spatial language

### INV-012194 | sec 1341 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA SALE | sintaxis de Seedream
- **Seedream** is the shortest, comma stack like Midjourney but no flags

### INV-012195 | sec 1341 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA SALE | Kling y modelos de video
- **Kling** (and every motion adapter) leads with camera motion (the stills adapters let it be implicit)

### INV-012196 | sec 1341 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA
Same intent. Different syntax. Same brand-lock and series-lock anchors flowing through all of them.

### INV-012197 | sec 1342 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el texto en pantalla no entra en ningún prompt
## What's not in any prompt

### INV-012198 | sec 1342 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el texto en pantalla no entra en ningún prompt
The on-screen text content. Even though shot_04 has an on-screen text overlay (`text_03`, "You don't have a content problem. You have an infrastructure problem."), none of the prompts contain that text. It's composited separately per the five-layer model.

### INV-012199 | sec 1342 | skills/visual-prompt-forge/examples/one-shot-all-adapters/README.md | MIXTA ENTRA | el texto en pantalla no entra en ningún prompt
This is the discipline: the image generator handles the image, the compositor handles the text, neither tries to do the other's job.
